function openTodayNightSupervisorDrawer(){
  const key=dateKey(today);
  const rows=(readonlyCache?.nightSupervisors||[]).filter(row=>String(row.date||'').slice(0,10)===key);
  $("#drawerKicker").textContent="TODAY NIGHT STUDY";$("#drawerTitle").textContent="오늘의 야자감독";
  const body=rows.length?rows.map(row=>`<div class="today-duty-person ${String(row.teacher||'').trim()===currentLoginTeacherName()?'mine':''}"><strong>${escapeHtml(row.teacher||'-')}</strong><span>${escapeHtml(row.place||'면학관 2~3층')}</span><small>${escapeHtml([row.start,row.end].filter(Boolean).join('~'))}</small></div>`).join(''):`<div class="night-supervisor-empty"><i>◇</i><b>오늘 야자감독이 없습니다</b><span>43_야자감독계획의 오늘 날짜가 비어 있습니다.</span></div>`;
  $("#drawerBody").innerHTML=`<div class="today-duty-list"><time>${escapeHtml(key)}</time>${body}</div><div class="drawer-actions"><button class="btn primary" data-reference="night-supervisor">빠른열기 · 야자감독 전체 보기</button></div>`;
  $("#drawer").classList.remove("hidden");$("#drawerBackdrop").classList.remove("hidden");
  $("#drawerBody [data-reference='night-supervisor']")?.addEventListener("click",()=>openReferenceDrawer("night-supervisor"));
}

function dashboardDutyBadgesMarkup(){
  const lunch=dashboardDutyStatus("lunch");
  const night=dashboardDutyStatus("night");
  const badge=(kind,title,status,icon,reference)=>`<button class="uep-duty-badge ${status.state}" data-reference="${reference}" data-duty-kind="${kind}" data-quick-kind="lookup" title="${title} 상세 확인"><i>${icon}</i><span>${title}</span><b>${status.label}</b>${status.detail?`<small>${escapeHtml(status.detail)}</small>`:""}</button>`;
  return `<div class="uep-duty-badges">${badge("lunch","급식지도",lunch,"♨","lunch-supervisor-today")}${badge("night","야자감독",night,"◷","night-supervisor-today")}${dashboardStudentStatusCompactMarkup()}</div>`;
}

function timetableClassColorKey(room){
  const match=String(room||"").match(/(?:^|\b)([123])-?(\d{1,2})(?:\b|$)/);
  if(!match)return "";
  return `${match[1]}-${Number(match[2])}`;
}
function timetableClassColorClass(room){
  const key=timetableClassColorKey(room);
  if(!key)return "";
  let hash=0; for(const ch of key)hash=(hash*31+ch.charCodeAt(0))>>>0;
  return `class-color-${hash%9}`;
}


let dashboardTimetableMode="";
let studentTimetableSelectedId="";
function dashboardTimetableAutoMode(){
  if(dashboardTimetableMode)return dashboardTimetableMode;
  const now=new Date(),minute=now.getHours()*60+now.getMinutes();
  const end=(now.getDay()===3)?(14*60+30):(16*60+30);
  return minute>=end?"night":"day";
}
function dashboardNightWeekDates(){
  return timetableMasterWeekDates(dashboardWeekDate||dateKey(new Date()));
}
function dashboardNightAttendanceRows(dayKey,slot){
  return (readonlyCache?.nightAttendance||readonlyCache?.attendance||[]).filter(row=>{
    const key=String(row.date||row.day||"").slice(0,10);
    return key===dayKey&&nightSlotName(row)===slot&&nightRecordIsAttendance(row);
  });
}
function dashboardAfterProgramsForDay(dayKey){
  return (readonlyCache?.programs||[]).map(programWithOverride).filter(program=>{
    const text=`${program.kind||""} ${program.programType||""} ${program.afterType||""} ${program.type||""} ${program.title||""}`;
    const isAfter=/방과후|야간심화|심화수업/.test(text)||String(program.kind||"").toLowerCase()==="after";
    const pday=String(program.date||program.sessionDate||program.operationDate||"").slice(0,10);
    return isAfter&&programHasNightStudyImpact(program)&&pday===dayKey&&!/취소|휴강|결강/.test(String(program.status||program.operationState||""));
  });
}
function dashboardProgramNightSlot(program){
  const text=`${program.afterType||""} ${program.type||""} ${program.title||""} ${program.time||""}`;
  if(/야간심화/.test(text)){
    const m=String(program.time||"").match(/(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/); const minute=m?Number(m[1])*60+Number(m[2]):0;
    return minute>=20*60+20?"야자2":"야자1";
  }
  return "오후자습";
}
function dashboardNightWeekMarkup(){
  const dates=dashboardNightWeekDates(),days=["월","화","수","목","금"],slots=[
    {key:"오후자습",label:"오후자습",time:schoolHourRange("오후자습")||"17:00~18:00"},
    {key:"야자1",label:"야자1",time:schoolHourRange("야자1")||"19:00~20:10"},
    {key:"야자2",label:"야자2",time:schoolHourRange("야자2")||"20:20~21:30"}
  ];
  const cells=[];
  slots.forEach(slot=>{
    cells.push(`<div class="dashboard-night-period"><b>${escapeHtml(slot.label)}</b><small>${escapeHtml(slot.time)}</small></div>`);
    dates.forEach((date,i)=>{
      const key=dateKey(date),isWed=i===2,holiday=attendanceIsSchoolHoliday(key);
      if(holiday){cells.push(`<div class="dashboard-night-cell closed holiday"><b>미운영</b><small>공휴일</small></div>`);return;}
      if(isWed){cells.push(`<div class="dashboard-night-cell closed"><b>-</b><small>야간 미운영</small></div>`);return;}
      const rows=dashboardNightAttendanceRows(key,slot.key),students=new Set(rows.map(attendanceStudentKey).filter(Boolean));
      const programs=dashboardAfterProgramsForDay(key).filter(p=>dashboardProgramNightSlot(p)===slot.key);
      const programCards=programs.map(p=>`<button class="dashboard-night-program-chip" data-dashboard-night-program="${escapeHtml(p.id||p.programId||"")}" data-dashboard-night-program-day="${key}"><b>${/야간심화/.test(`${p.afterType||""} ${p.title||""}`)?"야간심화":"방과후"}</b><span>${escapeHtml(p.actualTitle||p.title||"프로그램")}</span></button>`).join("");
      cells.push(`<div class="dashboard-night-cell ${students.size||programs.length?'active':'empty'}"><button class="dashboard-night-roster" data-dashboard-night-day="${key}" data-dashboard-night-slot="${escapeHtml(slot.key)}"><b>${students.size?`${students.size}명`:'-'}</b><small>${students.size?'자습인원 보기':'자습 자료 없음'}</small></button>${programCards}</div>`);
    });
  });
  return `<div class="dashboard-night-week"><div class="dashboard-night-corner">시간대</div>${days.map((d,i)=>`<div class="dashboard-night-head"><b>${d}</b><small>${dates[i]?`${dates[i].getMonth()+1}/${dates[i].getDate()}`:""}</small></div>`).join("")}${cells.join("")}</div>`;
}

}

  $("#drawer").classList.remove("hidden");$("#drawerBackdrop").classList.remove("hidden");

      const programs=dashboardAfterProgramsForDay(key).filter(p=>dashboardProgramNightSlot(p)===slot.key);
      const programCards=programs.map(p=>`<button class="dashboard-night-program-chip" data-dashboard-night-program="${escapeHtml(p.id||p.programId||"")}" data-dashboard-night-program-day="${key}"><b>${/야간심화/.test(`${p.afterType||""} ${p.title||""}`)?"야간심화":"방과후"}</b><span>${escapeHtml(p.actualTitle||p.title||"프로그램")}</span></button>`).join("");
      cells.push(`<div class="dashboard-night-cell ${students.size||programs.length?'active':'empty'}"><button class="dashboard-night-roster" data-dashboard-night-day="${key}" data-dashboard-night-slot="${escapeHtml(slot.key)}"><b>${students.size?`${students.size}명`:'-'}</b><small>${students.size?'자습인원 보기':'자습 자료 없음'}</small></button>${programCards}</div>`);
    });
  });
  return `<div class="dashboard-night-week"><div class="dashboard-night-corner">시간대</div>${days.map((d,i)=>`<div class="dashboard-night-head"><b>${d}</b><small>${dates[i]?`${dates[i].getMonth()+1}/${dates[i].getDate()}`:""}</small></div>`).join("")}${cells.join("")}</div>`;
}
function dashboardTimetableHeaderControlsMarkup(){
  const mode=dashboardTimetableAutoMode();
  const dates=dashboardNightWeekDates(),first=dates[0],last=dates[4];
  const range=first&&last?`${first.getMonth()+1}/${first.getDate()}~${last.getMonth()+1}/${last.getDate()}`:"이번 주";
  return `<nav class="dashboard-timetable-header-controls"><button class="${mode==='day'?'active':''}" data-dashboard-timetable-mode="day">정규시간</button><button class="${mode==='night'?'active':''}" data-dashboard-timetable-mode="night">야간시간</button><span class="dashboard-tt-week-nav"><button data-dashboard-tt-week-shift="-1" aria-label="이전 주">‹</button><b>${range}</b><button data-dashboard-tt-week-shift="1" aria-label="다음 주">›</button></span></nav>`;
}
function dashboardTimetablePanelMarkup(){
  const mode=dashboardTimetableAutoMode();
  return mode==='night'?dashboardNightWeekMarkup():dashboardPersonalWeekMarkup();
}
function dashboardNightStudentGroups(day,slot){
  const rows=dashboardNightAttendanceRows(day,slot).map(normalizeDashboardStudentRow);
  const map=new Map();
  rows.forEach(row=>{
    const gc=recordGradeClass(row.studentRef||row),cls=String(Number(gc.classNo||0)||"");
    if(!cls)return;
    if(!map.has(cls))map.set(cls,new Map());
    const key=String(row.studentRef?.id||row.studentId||row.studentNo||`${row.name}|${cls}`);
    map.get(cls).set(key,row);
  });
  return map;
}
function openDashboardNightProgram(programId,day){
  const program=dashboardAfterProgramsForDay(day).find(p=>String(p.id||p.programId||"")===String(programId));
  if(!program)return toast("프로그램 정보를 찾지 못했습니다.");
  const isNight=/야간심화/.test(`${program.afterType||""} ${program.type||""} ${program.title||""}`);
  const students=Array.isArray(program.students)?program.students:[];
  $("#drawerKicker").textContent=isNight?"NIGHT ADVANCED":"AFTER SCHOOL"; $("#drawerTitle").textContent=program.actualTitle||program.title||"프로그램";
  $("#drawerBody").innerHTML=`<div class="history-summary positive"><span><small>운영일</small><b>${escapeHtml(day)}</b></span><span><small>시간</small><b>${escapeHtml(program.time||"확인 필요")}</b></span><span><small>참여</small><b>${students.length}명</b></span></div><div class="connection-detail"><div><b>구분</b><span>${isNight?"야간심화":"방과후"}</span></div><div><b>교과</b><span>${escapeHtml(program.subject||"-")}</span></div><div><b>담당교사</b><span>${escapeHtml(program.teacher||"-")}</span></div><div><b>장소</b><span>${escapeHtml(program.place||"-")}</span></div></div><div class="drawer-actions"><button class="btn primary" data-dashboard-night-program-detail="${escapeHtml(program.id||program.programId||"")}">상세보기</button></div>`;
  $("#drawer").classList.remove("hidden");$("#drawerBackdrop").classList.remove("hidden");
  $("[data-dashboard-night-program-detail]")?.addEventListener("click",()=>openAfterschoolProgramDrawer(program.id||program.programId));
}

function dashboardDutyBadgesMarkup(){
  const lunch=dashboardDutyStatus("lunch");
  const night=dashboardDutyStatus("night");
  const badge=(kind,title,status,icon,reference)=>`<button class="uep-duty-badge ${status.state}" data-reference="${reference}" data-duty-kind="${kind}" data-quick-kind="lookup" title="${title} 상세 확인"><i>${icon}</i><span>${title}</span><b>${status.label}</b>${status.detail?`<small>${escapeHtml(status.detail)}</small>`:""}</button>`;
  return `<div class="uep-duty-badges">${badge("lunch","급식지도",lunch,"♨","lunch-supervisor-today")}${badge("night","야자감독",night,"◷","night-supervisor-today")}${dashboardStudentStatusCompactMarkup()}</div>`;
}

function timetableClassColorKey(room){
  const match=String(room||"").match(/(?:^|\b)([123])-?(\d{1,2})(?:\b|$)/);
  if(!match)return "";
  return `${match[1]}-${Number(match[2])}`;
}
function timetableClassColorClass(room){
  const key=timetableClassColorKey(room);
  if(!key)return "";
  let hash=0; for(const ch of key)hash=(hash*31+ch.charCodeAt(0))>>>0;
  return `class-color-${hash%9}`;
}


let dashboardTimetableMode="";
let studentTimetableSelectedId="";
function dashboardTimetableAutoMode(){
  if(dashboardTimetableMode)return dashboardTimetableMode;
  const now=new Date(),minute=now.getHours()*60+now.getMinutes();
  const end=(now.getDay()===3)?(14*60+30):(16*60+30);
  return minute>=end?"night":"day";
}
function dashboardNightWeekDates(){
  return timetableMasterWeekDates(dashboardWeekDate||dateKey(new Date()));
}
function dashboardNightAttendanceRows(dayKey,slot){
  return (readonlyCache?.nightAttendance||readonlyCache?.attendance||[]).filter(row=>{
    const key=String(row.date||row.day||"").slice(0,10);
    return key===dayKey&&nightSlotName(row)===slot&&nightRecordIsAttendance(row);
  });
}
function dashboardAfterProgramsForDay(dayKey){
  return (readonlyCache?.programs||[]).map(programWithOverride).filter(program=>{
    const text=`${program.kind||""} ${program.programType||""} ${program.afterType||""} ${program.type||""} ${program.title||""}`;
    const isAfter=/방과후|야간심화|심화수업/.test(text)||String(program.kind||"").toLowerCase()==="after";
    const pday=String(program.date||program.sessionDate||program.operationDate||"").slice(0,10);
    return isAfter&&programHasNightStudyImpact(program)&&pday===dayKey&&!/취소|휴강|결강/.test(String(program.status||program.operationState||""));
  });
}
function dashboardProgramNightSlot(program){
  const text=`${program.afterType||""} ${program.type||""} ${program.title||""} ${program.time||""}`;
  if(/야간심화/.test(text)){
    const m=String(program.time||"").match(/(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/); const minute=m?Number(m[1])*60+Number(m[2]):0;
    return minute>=20*60+20?"야자2":"야자1";
  }
  return "오후자습";
}
function dashboardNightWeekMarkup(){
  const dates=dashboardNightWeekDates(),days=["월","화","수","목","금"],slots=[
    {key:"오후자습",label:"오후자습",time:schoolHourRange("오후자습")||"17:00~18:00"},
    {key:"야자1",label:"야자1",time:schoolHourRange("야자1")||"19:00~20:10"},
    {key:"야자2",label:"야자2",time:schoolHourRange("야자2")||"20:20~21:30"}
  ];
  const cells=[];
  slots.forEach(slot=>{
    cells.push(`<div class="dashboard-night-period"><b>${escapeHtml(slot.label)}</b><small>${escapeHtml(slot.time)}</small></div>`);
    dates.forEach((date,i)=>{
      const key=dateKey(date),isWed=i===2,holiday=attendanceIsSchoolHoliday(key);
      if(holiday){cells.push(`<div class="dashboard-night-cell closed holiday"><b>미운영</b><small>공휴일</small></div>`);return;}
      if(isWed){cells.push(`<div class="dashboard-night-cell closed"><b>-</b><small>야간 미운영</small></div>`);return;}
      const rows=dashboardNightAttendanceRows(key,slot.key),students=new Set(rows.map(attendanceStudentKey).filter(Boolean));
      const programs=dashboardAfterProgramsForDay(key).filter(p=>dashboardProgramNightSlot(p)===slot.key);
      const programCards=programs.map(p=>`<button class="dashboard-night-program-chip" data-dashboard-night-program="${escapeHtml(p.id||p.programId||"")}" data-dashboard-night-program-day="${key}"><b>${/야간심화/.test(`${p.afterType||""} ${p.title||""}`)?"야간심화":"방과후"}</b><span>${escapeHtml(p.actualTitle||p.title||"프로그램")}</span></button>`).join("");
      cells.push(`<div class="dashboard-night-cell ${students.size||programs.length?'active':'empty'}"><button class="dashboard-night-roster" data-dashboard-night-day="${key}" data-dashboard-night-slot="${escapeHtml(slot.key)}"><b>${students.size?`${students.size}명`:'-'}</b><small>${students.size?'자습인원 보기':'자습 자료 없음'}</small></button>${programCards}</div>`);
    });
  });
  return `<div class="dashboard-night-week"><div class="dashboard-night-corner">시간대</div>${days.map((d,i)=>`<div class="dashboard-night-head"><b>${d}</b><small>${dates[i]?`${dates[i].getMonth()+1}/${dates[i].getDate()}`:""}</small></div>`).join("")}${cells.join("")}</div>`;
}

  $("#drawerBody").innerHTML=`<div class="history-summary positive"><span><small>운영일</small><b>${escapeHtml(day)}</b></span><span><small>시간</small><b>${escapeHtml(program.time||"확인 필요")}</b></span><span><small>참여</small><b>${students.length}명</b></span></div><div class="connection-detail"><div><b>구분</b><span>${isNight?"야간심화":"방과후"}</span></div><div><b>교과</b><span>${escapeHtml(program.subject||"-")}</span></div><div><b>담당교사</b><span>${escapeHtml(program.teacher||"-")}</span></div><div><b>장소</b><span>${escapeHtml(program.place||"-")}</span></div></div><div class="drawer-actions"><button class="btn primary" data-dashboard-night-program-detail="${escapeHtml(program.id||program.programId||"")}">상세보기</button></div>`;
  $("#drawer").classList.remove("hidden");$("#drawerBackdrop").classList.remove("hidden");
  $("[data-dashboard-night-program-detail]")?.addEventListener("click",()=>openAfterschoolProgramDrawer(program.id||program.programId));

    $("#drawer").classList.remove("hidden");$("#drawerBackdrop").classList.remove("hidden");

function shiftDateKey(key, delta) {
  /* UEP_08197_ATTENDANCE_DATE_SHIFT */
  const match = String(key || "").match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/);
  if (!match) return dateKey(today);
  const year = Number(match[1]), month = Number(match[2]), day = Number(match[3]);
  const date = new Date(year, month - 1, day + Number(delta || 0), 12, 0, 0, 0);
  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return dateKey(today);
  return date.getFullYear() + "-" + String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0") + "-" + String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, "0");
}
function attendanceDateControls(label,actionHtml="") {
  return `<div class="attendance-date-controls"><span>${escapeHtml(label)}</span><button class="btn secondary" data-attendance-date-step="-1">‹</button><div class="attendance-date-input-wrap"><input type="date" id="attendanceViewDate" value="${escapeHtml(attendanceViewDate)}"><strong>${escapeHtml(weekdayLabelFromKey(attendanceViewDate, true))}</strong></div><button class="btn secondary" data-attendance-date-step="1">›</button><button class="btn secondary" data-attendance-today>오늘</button>${actionHtml?`<div class="attendance-date-action">${actionHtml}</div>`:""}</div>`;
}
function nightSlotName(rowOrText) {
  const text = typeof rowOrText === "string" ? rowOrText : `${rowOrText?.time || ""} ${rowOrText?.type || ""} ${rowOrText?.detail || ""}`;
  // 운호고 방과후 8교시는 오후자습 시간대이다.
  if (/오후자습|1타임|8교시/.test(text)) return "오후자습";
  if (/야자\s*1|야간\s*1|2타임/.test(text)) return "야자1";
  if (/야자\s*2|야간\s*2|3타임/.test(text)) return "야자2";
  return String(rowOrText?.time || rowOrText?.type || text || "기타").trim();
}
function minutesOfClock(value) {
  const match = String(value || "").match(/(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/);
  return match ? Number(match[1]) * 60 + Number(match[2]) : null;
}
function slotRange(slot) {
  return {"오후자습":[17*60,18*60],"야자1":[19*60+20,20*60+10],"야자2":[20*60+20,21*60+30]}[slot] || null;
}
function isWednesdayKey(value) {
  const key=String(value||"").slice(0,10);
  if(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(key)){
    const d=new Date(`${key}T12:00:00`);
    return !Number.isNaN(d.getTime())&&d.getDay()===3;
  }
  return /^수(?:요일)?$/.test(String(value||"").trim());
}
function programHasNightStudyImpact(program) {
  const dates=[program?.date,...(Array.isArray(program?.dates)?program.dates:[])].filter(Boolean);
  if(dates.length&&dates.every(isWednesdayKey)) return false;
  const weekdayText=String(program?.weekdays||program?.weekday||program?.day||"").replace(/\s/g,"");
  if(weekdayText&&/^(수|수요일)$/.test(weekdayText)) return false;
  // UEP_08177_AFTER_SCHOOL_NIGHT_LINK_ONLY: 문자열 N을 Boolean(N)으로 처리하지 않는다.
  const raw=program?.affectsAttendance??program?.nightLinked??program?.nightLink??program?.['야자연계여부'];
  if(raw===true)return true;
  if(raw===false||raw==null)return false;
  return /^(Y|YES|TRUE|1|예|연계|적용)$/i.test(String(raw).trim());
}
function singlePeriodLabel(session) {
  const candidates=[session?.period,session?.startTime,session?.time].map(v=>String(v||"").trim()).filter(Boolean);
  for(const text of candidates){
    const m=text.match(/(\d+)교시/);
    if(m) return `${m[1]}교시`;
  }
  return String(session?.time||session?.period||"시간 확인");
}
function programOverlapsSlot(program, slot) {
  if(isWednesdayKey(program?.date)||!programHasNightStudyImpact(program)) return false;
  const descriptor = `${program?.time || ""} ${program?.title || ""} ${program?.actualTitle || ""} ${program?.type || ""}`;
  // 차시 시작시각이 8교시라면 시트의 기존 야자영향타임 값보다 학교 운영 기준을 우선한다.
  // 즉 8교시는 야자1이 아니라 오후자습으로만 표시한다.
  if (/8교시/.test(descriptor)) return slot === "오후자습";
  const explicitSlots=(program?.nightSlots||[]).flatMap(value=>{
    const text=String(value||"");
    return ["오후자습","야자1","야자2"].filter(name=>text.includes(name));
  });
  if(explicitSlots.length) return explicitSlots.includes(slot);
  const range = slotRange(slot);
  const namedSlot = nightSlotName(descriptor);
  if (namedSlot && ["오후자습","야자1","야자2"].includes(namedSlot) && !String(namedSlot).includes("기타")) return namedSlot === slot;
  const clocks = descriptor.match(/\d{1,2}:\d{2}/g) || [];
  if (!range || clocks.length < 2) return Boolean(program?.affectsAttendance && /오후자습/.test(descriptor) && slot === "오후자습");
  const start = minutesOfClock(clocks[0]), end = minutesOfClock(clocks[1]);
  return Number.isFinite(start) && Number.isFinite(end) && start < range[1] && end > range[0];
}
function programParticipationFor(student, day, slot) {
  const id = String(student?.id || student?.studentId || "");
  const number = String(student?.studentNo || "");
  const connected = (readonlyCache?.programs || []).filter(program=>program?.kind!=="after");
  const derived = typeof afterSchoolProgramGroups === "function" ? afterSchoolProgramGroups() : [];
  const seen = new Set();
  const programs = [...connected, ...derived].filter(program => {
    const key = `${program.id || ""}|${program.date || ""}|${program.title || ""}|${program.time || ""}`;
    if (seen.has(key)) return false; seen.add(key); return true;
  });
  for (const program of programs) {
    if(isWholeSchoolCommonActivity(program))continue;
    if (program.date !== day || !programOverlapsSlot(program, slot)) continue;
    const target = (program.students || []).find(item => String(item.studentId || "") === id || (number && String(item.studentNo || item.number || "") === number));
    if (!target) continue;
    const participationText = [target.result,target.attendance,target.participation,target.status,target.checkMethod].filter(Boolean).join(" ");
    const inactive = /결석|불참|취소|중도포기|퇴강|미참여/.test(participationText);
    // 13_출석부의 활성 수강명단은 출석상태가 '등록'이어도 해당 실제 차시에는 프로그램 참여자로 본다.
    // 실제 수업일·야자영향타임은 program.date/program.time 단계에서 이미 검증되므로 명단 등록만으로 다른 날짜에 표시되지 않는다.
    const confirmed = !inactive && ((typeof nightRecordIsAttendance === "function" && nightRecordIsAttendance(target)) || /출석|정상|참여|입실|명단QR|학사|등록|신청/.test(participationText));
    return {program, target, confirmed, pending: !confirmed && !inactive};
  }
  return null;
}
function nightProgramStatus(program){
  const text=`${program?.afterType||""} ${program?.type||""} ${program?.actualTitle||program?.title||""}`;
  if(/야간심화|심화수업/.test(text)) return {cls:"after",label:"야간심화",kind:"after"};
  if(/일반 방과후|방학 방과후|방과후학교/.test(text)) return {cls:"after",label:"방과후",kind:"after"};
  const title=String(program?.actualTitle||program?.title||"프로그램");
  return {cls:"program",label:title.length>8?`${title.slice(0,7)}…`:title,kind:program?.kind||"general"};
}
function attendanceIsWeekday(day){const n=new Date(`${day}T12:00:00`).getDay();return n>=1&&n<=5;}
function attendanceIsSchoolHoliday(day){
  const key=String(day||'').slice(0,10);
  const fixed2026=new Set(['2026-01-01','2026-02-16','2026-02-17','2026-02-18','2026-03-01','2026-03-02','2026-05-05','2026-05-24','2026-05-25','2026-06-06','2026-08-15','2026-08-17','2026-09-24','2026-09-25','2026-09-26','2026-10-03','2026-10-05','2026-10-09','2026-12-25']);
  if(fixed2026.has(key))return true;
  const closureRx=/공휴일|대체공휴일|재량휴업|휴업일|개교기념일|임시공휴일|석가탄신일|부처님오신날|어린이날|현충일|광복절|개천절|한글날|성탄절|설날|추석/;
  return schoolEventsForDate(key).some(event=>{
    const title=String(event.title||'').trim(); const type=String(event.type||'').trim();
    if(!closureRx.test(title+' '+type))return false;
    if(/공휴일|대체공휴일|재량휴업|휴업일|개교기념일|임시공휴일/.test(title+' '+type))return true;
    const start=String(event.date||'').slice(0,10), end=String(event.endDate||event.date||'').slice(0,10);
    return start===key&&end===key;
  });
}
function isNightOperatingDay(day){return attendanceIsWeekday(day)&&!attendanceIsSchoolHoliday(day);}
function nearestNightOperatingDay(day){
  let d=new Date(`${day}T12:00:00`);
  for(let i=0;i<31;i++){const key=dateKey(d);if(isNightOperatingDay(key))return key;d.setDate(d.getDate()-1);}
  return day;
}
function shiftNightOperatingDate(day,direction){
  let d=new Date(`${day}T12:00:00`);
  const step=Number(direction)>=0?1:-1;
  for(let i=0;i<62;i++){
    d.setDate(d.getDate()+step);
    const key=dateKey(d);
    if(isNightOperatingDay(key))return key;
  }
  return day;
}

    const roster=(readonlyCache?.dormStudents||[]).find(item=>ids.includes(String(item.studentId||""))||ids.includes(String(item.studentNo||"")));
    if(!roster) return false;
    const entered=String(roster.entryDate||roster.startDate||roster.moveInDate||"2026-07-20").slice(0,10);
    const exited=String(roster.exitDate||roster.endDate||roster.moveOutDate||"").slice(0,10);
    return day>=entered && (!exited||day<=exited);
  };

  const rowIndex = new Map();
  monthRows.forEach(row=>{
    const slot=nightSlotName(row); if(!slots.includes(slot)||!row.date) return;
    const ids=[String(row.studentId||""),String(row.studentNo||""),attendanceStudentKey(row)].filter(Boolean);
    ids.forEach(id=>{const key=`${row.date}|${slot}|${id}`; if(!rowIndex.has(key)) rowIndex.set(key,row);});
  });
  const programIndex = new Map();
  const programs=[...(readonlyCache?.programs||[]).map(programWithOverride).filter(program=>program?.kind!=="after"),...(typeof afterSchoolProgramGroups==="function"?afterSchoolProgramGroups():[])];
  const monthDateCandidates=[...new Set([...(readonlyCache?.nightCalendarDates||[]),...monthRows.map(row=>row.date),attendanceViewDate].filter(day=>String(day||"").startsWith(nightViewMonth)))].sort();
  const weekdayToken=day=>["일","월","화","수","목","금","토"][new Date(`${day}T12:00:00`).getDay()];
  const programRunsOn=(program,day)=>{
    const statusText=`${program.status||""} ${program.changeType||""} ${program.sessionStatus||""}`;
    if(/결강|휴강|취소|폐강|미운영/.test(statusText)) return false;
    const start=String(program.date||program.startDate||"").slice(0,10);
    const end=String(program.endDate||"").slice(0,10);
    const explicitDates=[...(Array.isArray(program.dates)?program.dates:[]),...(Array.isArray(program.sessionDates)?program.sessionDates:[])].map(value=>String(value||"").slice(0,10)).filter(Boolean);
    const days=String(program.weekdays||program.scheduleText||"");
    if(program.kind==="after"){
      if(explicitDates.length) return explicitDates.includes(day);
      if(start&&end&&end!==start){
        if(day<start||day>end) return false;
        if(days&&/[월화수목금토일]/.test(days)&&!days.includes(weekdayToken(day))) return false;
        return true;
      }
      return Boolean(start&&start===day);
    }
    if(start&&day<start) return false;
    if(end&&day>end) return false;
    if(days&&/[월화수목금토일]/.test(days) && !days.includes(weekdayToken(day))) return false;
    return start===day || (start&&end&&day>=start&&day<=end);
  };
  const seenPrograms=new Set();
  programs.filter(program=>programHasNightStudyImpact(program)).forEach(program=>{
    const pKey=`${program.id||""}|${program.date||""}|${program.title||""}|${program.time||""}`;
    if(seenPrograms.has(pKey)) return; seenPrograms.add(pKey);
    monthDateCandidates.filter(day=>programRunsOn(program,day)).forEach(day=>{
      slots.filter(slot=>programOverlapsSlot(program,slot)).forEach(slot=>{
        (program.students||[]).forEach(target=>{
          const result=String(target.result||"대상");
          const confirmed=nightRecordIsAttendance(target)||/출석|정상|참여|입실|명단QR|학사|등록|수강|대상/.test(result);
          const pending=!confirmed&&!/결석|불참|취소/.test(result);
          const value={program,target,confirmed,pending};
          const ids=[String(target.studentId||""),String(target.studentNo||target.number||""),attendanceStudentKey(target)].filter(Boolean);
          ids.forEach(id=>{
            const key=`${day}|${slot}|${id}`, prev=programIndex.get(key);
            if(!prev||confirmed||(!prev.confirmed&&pending)) programIndex.set(key,value);
          });
        });
      });
    });
  });
  const resolveStatus=(student,day,slot)=>{
    const ids=[String(student.id||student.studentId||""),String(student.studentNo||""),attendanceStudentKey(student)].filter(Boolean);
    let participation=null,row=null;
    for(const id of ids){ participation=participation||programIndex.get(`${day}|${slot}|${id}`); row=row||rowIndex.get(`${day}|${slot}|${id}`); }
    if(participation?.confirmed){
      const display=nightProgramStatus(participation.program);
      const fullTitle=participation.program.actualTitle||participation.program.title||"프로그램";
      return {cls:display.cls,label:display.label,title:`${fullTitle} 참여 · ${participation.program.subject||"교과 미입력"} · ${participation.program.department||"부서 미입력"}`,programId:participation.program.id||"",programKind:display.kind};
    }
    if(participation?.pending) return {cls:"pending",label:"확인",title:`${participation.program.actualTitle||participation.program.title} 대상·출석·참여 중`};
    if(row){
      const rowText=`${row.result||""} ${row.type||""} ${row.affiliation||""} ${row.detail||""}`;
      if(row.recognizedByProgram||/프로그램 인정/.test(String(row.result))){const display=nightProgramStatus({kind:row.programKind,afterType:row.programType,type:row.type,actualTitle:row.programTitle,title:row.programTitle});return {cls:display.cls,label:display.label,title:`${row.programTitle||"프로그램"} 참여`,programId:row.programId||"",programKind:display.kind};}
      if(row.programPending||/프로그램 확인/.test(String(row.result))) return {cls:"pending",label:"확인",title:`${row.programTitle||"프로그램"} 참여 확인 필요`};
      if(/결석|미대상|명단외|미입실|미제출|불참/.test(rowText)) return {cls:"none",label:"-",title:"참여 기록 없음"};
      if(/방과후|야간모의|방학/.test(rowText) && nightRecordIsAttendance(row)) return {cls:"after",label:"방과후",title:String(row.type||row.result||"방과후 참여")};
      if(/프로그램/.test(rowText) && nightRecordIsAttendance(row)) return {cls:"program",label:"프로그램",title:String(row.type||row.result||"프로그램 참여")};
      if(/지각/.test(String(row.result))) return {cls:"late",label:"지각",title:String(row.result)};
      if(nightRecordIsAttendance(row)){
        const isDorm=/학사/.test(rowText)||isDormForDay(student,day);
        return isDorm?{cls:"dorm",label:"학사",title:"학사생 참여"}:{cls:"present",label:"출석",title:String(row.result||"출석")};
      }
    }
    if(isDormForDay(student,day)) return {cls:"dorm",label:"학사",title:day<="2026-07-16"?"1학기 학사생 자동 표시"

':'추가 수정 없음'}</small></div>${profilePanel}${errorPanel}<div class="curriculum-term-grid">${['2학년 1학기','2학년 2학기','3학년 1학기','3학년 2학기'].map(term=>{const rows=byTerm.get(term)||[];return `<section><header><h3>${escapeHtml(term)}</h3><span>${rows.length}과목</span></header>${rows.map(x=>`<article><b>${escapeHtml(x.subject)}</b><small>${escapeHtml([x.group,x.track,x.major].filter(Boolean).join(' · '))}</small><em>${escapeHtml(x.status||'상담용 신청')}</em></article>`).join('')||'<div class="empty-inline">신청 없음</div>'}</section>`;}).join('')}</div>${curriculumMessagePanel(student,bundle)}`;
  }
  return recordWriterWorkspace(student,bundle);
}
function recordsView() {
  const allStudents=readonlyCache?.students||[];
  const classStudents=allStudents.filter(s=>recordStudentClass(s)===String(recordClassNo)).sort((a,b)=>Number(a.number||0)-Number(b.number||0));
  if(!recordStudentId||!allStudents.some(s=>s.id===recordStudentId))recordStudentId=(classStudents[0]||allStudents[0])?.id||"";
  let student=allStudents.find(s=>s.id===recordStudentId)||classStudents[0]||allStudents[0];
  if(student&&recordStudentClass(student)!==String(recordClassNo))recordClassNo=recordStudentClass(student)||recordClassNo;
  const visibleClassStudents=allStudents.filter(s=>recordStudentClass(s)===String(recordClassNo)).filter(s=>!(recordMode==='curriculum'&&recordCurriculumErrorOnly)||selectionErrorsForStudent(s).length>0).sort((a,b)=>Number(a.number||0)-Number(b.number||0));
  if(!visibleClassStudents.some(s=>s.id===student?.id))student=visibleClassStudents[0]||student;
  const bundle=student?studentRecordBundle(student):{activities:[],selected:[],finalRecords:[],programs:[],reports:[]};
  const tabs=[["activities","활동내역"],["sdgs","SDGs 연계 성장 프로파일"],["curriculum","교육과정"],["final","기록"]];
  const classCards=Array.from({length:9},(_,i)=>String(i+1)).map(no=>`<button data-record-class="${no}" class="${String(recordClassNo)===no?"active":""}"><b>${no}반</b><span>${allStudents.filter(s=>recordStudentClass(s)===no).length}명</span></button>`).join("");
  const options=visibleClassStudents.map(s=>`<option value="${escapeHtml(s.id)}" ${s.id===student?.id?"selected":""}>${escapeHtml(s.studentNo)} ${escapeHtml(s.name)}</option>`).join("");
  const personalToolbar=`<div class="record-student-toolbar personal-only"><div class="record-class-cards">${classCards}</div><label>${recordClassNo}반 학생<select id="recordStudentSelect">${options}</select></label><button class="btn primary" data-open-student-dashboard="${escapeHtml(student?.id||"")}">학생 개인 대시보드</button></div>`;
  let content="";
  if(recordMode==="sdgs"){
    content=personalToolbar+recordPersonalContent(student,bundle);
  }else if(recordQueryMode==="class"){
    content=`<div class="record-query-bar"><div class="record-query-switch"><button data-record-query="class" class="active">반별 조회</button><button data-record-query="student">개인별 조회</button></div><div class="record-class-cards">${classCards}</div>${recordMode==='curriculum'?`<button type="button" class="btn ${recordCurriculumErrorOnly?'primary':'secondary'}" data-curriculum-error-only>${recordCurriculumErrorOnly?'오류학생만 보는 중':'오류학생만 보기'}</button><button type="button" class="btn secondary" data-generate-bulk-curriculum-message>오류학생 일괄 문구 생성</button>`:''}</div><div class="record-class-summary"><div><small>${recordMode==="activities"?"ACTIVITY":recordMode==="curriculum"?"CURRICULUM":"FINAL RECORD"}</small><h3>${recordClassNo}반 한 번에 보기</h3><p>${recordMode==='curriculum'?'오류 배지를 누르면 해당 학생의 신청내역과 안내문을 확인할 수 있습니다.':'학생을 누르면 개인 상세로 전환됩니다.'}</p></div><span>${visibleClassStudents.length}명</span></div>${recordClassTable(visibleClassStudents)}${recordMode==='curriculum'?`<section class="curriculum-message-panel curriculum-bulk-panel" id="curriculumBulkPanel" hidden><header><div><small>BULK MESSAGE</small><h3>${recordClassNo}반 오류학생 일괄 안내문</h3></div><span>${visibleClassStudents.filter(s=>selectionErrorsForStudent(s).length).length}명</span></header><textarea id="curriculumBulkMessageText" readonly></textarea><div class="curriculum-message-actions"><button type="button" class="btn primary" data-copy-bulk-curriculum-message>전체 복사</button><a class="btn secondary" href="${escapeHtml(SUBJECT_REAPPLICATION_FORM_URL)}" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">수강신청 구글폼 열기</a></div><p>현재 반의 오류학생 안내문을 학번·이름 구분선과 함께 한 번에 생성합니다.</p></section>`:''}`;
  }else{
    content=`<div class="record-query-bar"><div class="record-query-switch"><button data-record-query="class">반별 조회</button><button data-record-query="student" class="active">개인별 조회</button></div></div>${personalToolbar}${recordPersonalContent(student,bundle)}`;
  }
  return `<div class="module-page records-v0601"><div class="record-main-tabs">${tabs.map(([id,label])=>`<button data-record-mode="${id}" class="${recordMode===id?"active":""}"><b>${label}</b><small>${id==="activities"?"학생 활동 사실 확인":id==="sdgs"?"진로·전공·탐구주제·사회적 가치 연계":id==="curriculum"?"학기별 선택과목":"학생 성장 자료"}</small></button>`).join("")}</div>${content}</div>`;
}
function afterSchoolProgramGroups() {
  const connected = (readonlyCache?.programs || []).map(programWithOverride).filter((program) => program.kind === "after").map((program) => ({...program, reportRequired:false}));
  const rows = (readonlyCache?.afterSchoolAttendance || []).slice();
  const groups = new Map();
  rows.forEach((row) => {
    const type = String(row.type || "방과후 프로그램").trim() || "방과후 프로그램";
    const time = String(row.time || row.period || "").trim();
    const key = [row.date || "", type, time].join("|");
    if (!groups.has(key)) groups.set(key, { key, id: `after-${encodeURIComponent(key)}`, date: row.date || "", title: type, actualTitle: type, recordTitle: type, time: time || "시간 미등록", place: row.detail || "운영정보 확인", teacher: "", records: [], kind: "after", reportRequired: false, affectsAttendance: /오후자습/.test(type), source:"30_야자출결_정규화" });
    groups.get(key).records.push(row);
  });
  const derived = [...groups.values()].map((program) => {
    const seen = new Set();
    program.students = program.records.filter((row) => {
      const key = attendanceStudentKey(row);
      if (seen.has(key)) return false;
      seen.add(key);
      return true;
    });
    program.attendedCount = program.students.filter(nightRecordIsAttendance).length;
    return program;
  });

  const cleanId=value=>String(value||"").replace(/^after-(?:course|master|session)-/,"").trim();
  const courseKey=program=>cleanId(program?.courseId||program?.programId||program?.id||"");
  const slotKey=program=>{
    const named=typeof nightSlotName==="function"?nightSlotName(program):"";
    if(["오후자습","야자1","야자2"].includes(named)) return named;
    return String(program?.time||program?.period||"").replace(/\s/g,"").trim()||"시간미등록";
  };
  const eventKey=program=>{
    const sid=cleanId(program?.sessionId||"");
    if(sid) return `session|${sid}`;
    const course=courseKey(program);
    const date=String(program?.date||"").slice(0,10);
    const slot=slotKey(program);
    const title=String(program?.actualTitle||program?.title||program?.type||"").replace(/\s/g,"").trim();
    return ["event",course,date,slot,title].join("|");
  };

  // 강좌 마스터와 실제 차시가 함께 들어온 경우 차시가 일정의 원본이다.
  const sessionCourseKeys=new Set(connected.filter(program=>!program.isCourseMaster).map(courseKey).filter(Boolean));
  const canonicalConnected=[];
  const seenConnected=new Set();
  connected.forEach(program=>{
    if(program.isCourseMaster&&courseKey(program)&&sessionCourseKeys.has(courseKey(program))) return;
    const key=eventKey(program);
    if(seenConnected.has(key)) return;
    seenConnected.add(key);
    canonicalConnected.push(program);
  });

  // 실제 차시가 존재하는 날짜·타임에서는 출결 정규화 행으로 두 번째 일정 카드를 만들지 않는다.
  const connectedCoverage=new Set(canonicalConnected.filter(program=>!program.isCourseMaster&&program.date).map(program=>`${String(program.date).slice(0,10)}|${slotKey(program)}`));
  const canonicalDerived=derived.filter(program=>!connectedCoverage.has(`${String(program.date||"").slice(0,10)}|${slotKey(program)}`));

  return [...canonicalConnected, ...canonicalDerived].sort((a,b) => String(b.date).localeCompare(String(a.date)) || String(a.title).localeCompare(String(b.title)));
}
function groupedAfterSchoolCourses(items) {
  const groups=new Map();
  (items||[]).filter(p=>p&&p.kind==="after").forEach(program=>{
    const courseId=String(program.courseId||program.programId||program.id||"").replace(/^after-session-/,"");
    const key=courseId||`${program.actualTitle||program.title}|${program.type||""}`;
    if(!groups.has(key)) groups.set(key,{...program,id:`after-course-${key}`,sessionIds:[],dates:[],students:[],attendedCount:0,sessionCount:0});
    const group=groups.get(key);
    if(!program.isCourseMaster) group.sessionIds.push(program.sessionId||program.id);
    if(program.date) group.dates.push(program.date);
    if(program.endDate) group.dates.push(program.endDate);
    if(!program.isCourseMaster) group.sessionCount+=1;
    group.affectsAttendance=Boolean(group.affectsAttendance||programHasNightStudyImpact(program));
    if((group.dates||[]).length&&(group.dates||[]).every(isWednesdayKey)) group.affectsAttendance=false;
    const studentMap=new Map((group.students||[]).map(st=>[String(st.studentId||st.studentNo||st.number),st]));
    (program.students||[]).forEach(st=>{const sk=String(st.studentId||st.studentNo||st.number);if(sk&&!studentMap.has(sk))studentMap.set(sk,st);});
    group.students=[...studentMap.values()];
  });
  return [...groups.values()].map(group=>{
    const dates=[...new Set(group.dates)].sort();
    const first=dates[0]||group.date||"", last=dates.at(-1)||first;
    group.date=first;

    group.attendedCount=group.students.filter(nightRecordIsAttendance).length;
    group.sessionNo=`${group.sessionCount}차시`;
    group.recordTitle=

  if(programPlanningMode==='programs')body=`<div class="planning-two-column"><section><h4>자율활동 · 보고서 기반 SDGs</h4>${usedSdgs.length?usedSdgs.map(([n,v])=>`<p class="planning-list-row"><span>SDG ${n} ${escapeHtml(v.name)}</span><b>${v.count}건</b></p>`).join(''):'<div class="empty-state">자율활동 보고서에서 분석된 SDGs가 없습니다.</div>'}<h4 class="planning-subtitle">가장 적은 SDGs</h4>${leastSdgs.map(([n,v])=>`<p class="planning-list-row planning-low"><span>SDG ${n} ${escapeHtml(v.name)}</span><b>${v.count}건</b></p>`).join('')}</section><section><h4>진로활동 · 계열 및 전공군</h4>${usedCareers.length?usedCareers.map(([,v])=>`<p class="planning-list-row"><span>${escapeHtml(v.label)}</span><b>${v.count}건</b></p>`).join(''):'<div class="empty-state">진로활동 보고서에서 분석된 계열 자료가 없습니다.</div>'}<h4 class="planning-subtitle">세부 전공군</h4>${majorCounts.size?[...majorCounts.entries()].sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1]).map(([k,v])=>`<p class="planning-list-row"><span>${escapeHtml(k)}</span><b>${v}건</b></p>`).join(''):'<div class="empty-state">분석된 전공군이 없습니다.</div>'}</section></div>`;
  if(programPlanningMode==='recommend')body=`<section class="planning-panel"><div class="planning-title-row"><div><h4>다음 프로그램 기획 근거</h4><p>프로그램 자체에 태그를 찍지 않고 학생 보고서의 자동 태그 결과를 근거로 부족 영역을 제시합니다.</p></div></div><div class="planning-recommendations">${recommendations.map((r,i)=>`<article><span>${i+1}</span><b>${escapeHtml(r)}</b></article>`).join('')}</div></section>`;
  return `<div class="program-planning-center"><div class="module-intro pastel-peach"><span>04</span><div><h3>프로그램 기획센터</h3><p>공통활동은 단순 현황으로만 확인하고, 자율활동은 학생 보고서의 SDGs, 진로활동은 계열·전공군, 방과후·야간심화는 교사별 운영시간을 근거로 다음 프로그램을 설계합니다.</p></div></div><div class="planning-tabs">${tabs}</div>${body}</div>`;
}

  const hub=`${programMonthPicker()}<div class="program-hub-grid program-hub-grid-fixed">${card("common","◉","공통활동","학교 창체운영계획","전체학생을 기본 대상으로 하고 출결 현황을 관리합니다.",commonSummary,"common")}${card("selected","★","선택활동","학년 특색프로그램","모집·참여학생, 자율·진로 영역, SDGs와 보고서를 함께 관리합니다.",selectedSummary,"selected")}${card("after","▦","방과후학교","수업·야자출결 연동","1학기·여름방학·2학기·겨울방학 수업과 수강학생을 관리합니다.",afterSummary,"after")}${card("planning","◎","프로그램 기획센터","다음 학기 설계","운영 이력·학생 수요·교사별 운영시간·SDGs 분포를 바탕으로 기획 근거를 확인합니다.",{count:commonMonth.length+selectedMont
--- match 5 at 859732 ---
otices.map(item=>item.source?`<button class="work-mini-item deadline" data-auto-deadline-source="${escapeHtml(item.source)}" data-auto-deadline-id="${escapeHtml(String(item.id||''))}"><span><b>${escapeHtml(item.title||'자동 마감공지')}</b><small>${escapeHtml(item.due||'')}</small></span><em>${deadlineBadge(item)}</em></button>`:`<button class="work-mini-item notice" data-work-notice-detail="${escapeHtml(item.id||'')}" data-work-notice-source="connected"><span><b>${escapeHtml(item.title||'학년공지')}</b><small>${escapeHtml(item.author||item.department||'1학년부')}</small></span><em>열기</em></button>`).join(''):'<div class="work-mini-empty">현재 게시 중인 학년공지가 없습니다.</div>';
  const reportRows=dashboardReportStatusRows().filter(row=>row.submitted===false),reportGroups=dashboardStatusStudentGroups(reportRows,{report:true});
  const classCount=classNo=>reportGroups.filter(row=>dashboardStatusClassNo(row)===String(classNo)).length;
  const own=dashboardOwnClassNo(),isHomeroom=currentRoleId()==='homeroom'&&own;
  const reportBody=isHomeroom?`<div class="dashboard-report-own-class">${reportGroups.filter(row=>dashboardStatusClassNo(row)===String(own)).map(row=>`<button data-dashboard-missing-student="${escapeHtml(row.key)}"><b>${escapeHtml(row.studentNo||'')} ${escapeHtml(row.name||'')}</b><small>${dashboardReportGroupState(row).submitted}/${dashboardReportGroupState(row).total}</small></button>`).join('')||'<div class="work-mini-empty">현재 미제출 학생이 없습니다.</div>'}</div>`:`<div class="dashboard-report-class-grid">${Array.from({length:9},(_,i)=>i+1).map(no=>`<button data-dashboard-missing-class="${no}"><b>${no}반</b><span>미제출 ${classCount(no)}명</span></button>`).join('')}</div>`;
  const checklistBody=checklist.length?checklist.map(item=>`<button class="work-mini-item checklist" data-work-edit="checklist" data-work-id="${escapeHtml(item.id||'')}"><span><b>${escapeHtml(item.title||'개인 업무')}</b><small>${escapeHtml(item.date||'기한 없음')}</small></span><em>미완료</em></button>`).join(''):'<div class="work-mini-empty">미완료 체크리스트가 없습니다.</div>';
  return `<article class="card task-card operations-card operations-card-v08212 widget-configurable" ${widgetAttrs('operations')}><div class="card-head"><div><span class="card-icon ci-blue">✓</span><strong>학년업무 · 학생점검</strong></div><div class="operation-head-tools"><button class="text-button notice-add-button" data-notice-add>＋ 공지</button><button class="text-button" data-page="work">내 체크리스트 ${checklist.length}</button></div></div><div class="work-three-grid"><section><header><b>학년공지</b><button data-page="work">전체</button></header><div class="work-panel-scroll">${noticeBody}</div></section><section><header><b>학생상담/점검</b><button data-student-counsel-route>전체</button></header>${dashboardCounselMarkup()}</section><section><header><b>보고서 미제출 현황</b><button data-dashboard-student-status="report">전체</button></header>${reportBody}</section></div><details class="dashboard-checklist-preserved"><summary>내 체크리스트 ${checklist.length}건</summary>${checklistBody}</details></article>`;

function normalizeProgramTitle(value) {
  return String(value || "").toLowerCase().replace(/[^0-9a-z가-힣]/g, "");
}

function dashboardTodayProgramVisible(program){
  if(!program)return false;
  const title=String(program.actualTitle||program.title||"").trim();
  const kind=String(program.kind||"");
  const target=String(program.target||program.targetText||program.audience||program.participantScope||program.targetGrade||"").replace(/\s/g,"");
  // 대시보드 표시를 명시적으로 끈 프로그램은 어떤 출처에서도 다시 살리지 않는다.
  if(program.showTodayProgram===false||program.showOnDashboard===false||program.dashboardVisible===false||program.dashboardShow===false)return false;
  // 개학식·방학식 등 학교 전체 의식/행사는 '프로그램' 카드가 아니다.
  if(/개학식|방학식|입학식|졸업식/.test(title))return false;
  if(kind==="calendar-first-grade")return calendarEventLooksLikeProgram(program)&&!isWholeSchoolDashboardCalendarEvent(program);
  if(isWholeSchoolCommonActivity(program))return false;
  // 공통활동 중 대상 텍스트가 전교생/전체학생/전학년이면 제외한다.
  if(kind==="common"&&/전교생|전체학생|전학년|학생전체|학교전체|1학년전체/.test(target))return false;
  // 공통활동의 참여명단이 현재 재학생 대부분을 덮는 경우도 전교생 자동참여로 본다.
  if(kind==="common"&&Array.isArray(program.students)&&program.students.length){
    const active=(readonlyCache?.students||[]).filter(studentIsCurrentlyActive);
    if(active.length>=20&&program.students.length>=Math.ceil(active.length*0.9))return false;
  }
  return true;
}

function todayProgramCollections(){
  const todayKey=dateKey(today);
  const connected=(readonlyCache?.programs||[]).map(programWithOverride).filter(program=>program.date===todayKey&&!isWholeSchoolCommonActivity(program)&&!(program.kind==="after"&&program.isCourseMaster));
  const connectedKeys=new Set(connected.map(program=>`${program.date}|${program.title}|${program.time}`));
  const local=(Array.isArray(state.programs)?state.programs:[]).filter(p=>p&&p.date===todayKey&&!connectedKeys.has(`${p.date}|${p.title}|${p.time}`)).map(p=>({...p,kind:"general",source:"이 PC 로컬"}));
  const afterGroups=afterSchoolProgramGroups().filter(p=>p.date===todayKey&&!p.isCourseMaster&&!connectedKeys.has(`${p.date}|${p.title}|${p.time}`));
  const all=[...connected,...local,...afterGroups];
  const knownTitles=all.map(item=>normalizeProgramTitle(item.actualTitle||item.title));
  // 학교캘린더에서 전교생 전체가 자동 참여하는 공통 일정은 오늘의 프로그램에 표시하지 않는다.
  // 09_공통활동마스터 자체를 제외하는 것이 아니라, 기존 학생 대상 공통·선택 프로그램은 그대로 유지한다.
  const calendarPrograms=schoolEventsForDate(todayKey).filter(event=>calendarEventTargetsFirstGrade(event)&&calendarEventLooksLikeProgram(event)&&!isWholeSchoolDashboardCalendarEvent(event)).filter(event=>{const title=normalizeProgramTitle(event.title);return !knownTitles.some(known=>known&&title&&(known.includes(title)||title.includes(known)));}).map(event=>({...event,kind:"calendar-first-grade"}));
  const selection=[...all.filter(p=>p.kind!=="after"),...calendarPrograms].filter(dashboardTodayProgramVisible).sort((a,b)=>String(a.time||"").localeCompare(String(b.time||"")));
  const after=all.filter(p=>p.kind==="after"||/방과후|야간심화|심화수업/.test(`${p.type||""} ${p.afterType||""} ${p.title||""}`)).filter(dashboardTodayProgramVisible).sort((a,b)=>String(a.time||"").localeCompare(String(b.time||"")));
  return {selection,after};
}
function dashboardProgramItem(p,group){
  if(p.kind==="calendar-first-grade")return `<button class="dashboard-program-row calendar-linked-program" data-school-event="${escapeHtml(p.id)}"><time>${escapeHtml(p.time||"종일")}</time><span><b>${escapeHtml(p.title)}</b><small>학교일정 연동 · ${escapeHtml(p.target||"1학년 대상")}${p.place?` · ${escapeHtml(p.place)}`:""}</small></span><em>일정 보기 ›</em></button>`;
  if(group==="after")return `<button class="dashboard-program-row program-area-after" data-after-program="${escapeHtml(p.id)}"><time>${escapeHtml(p.time||"시간 확인")}</time><span><b>${escapeHtml(p.actualTitle||p.title)}</b><small>${/야간심화/.test(`${p.afterType||""} ${p.title||""}`)?"야간심화":"방과후"} · ${escapeHtml(p.subject||"교과 미입력")} · ${escapeHtml(p.department||"부서 미입력")}</small></span><em>${Array.isArray(p.students)?p.students.length:0}명 ›</em></button>`;
  return `<button class="dashboard-program-row program-area-${programAreaKind(p)}" data-program="${escapeHtml(p.id)}"><time>${escapeHtml(p.time||"종일")}</time><span><b>${escapeHtml(p.actualTitle||p.title)}</b><small>${programAreaLabel(p)} · ${escapeHtml(p.place||"장소 확인")}</small></span><em>${Array.isArray(p.students)?p.students.length:0}명 ›</em></button>`;
}
function todayProgramsMarkup(){
  const {selection,after}=todayProgramCollections();
  return `<article class="card program-card dashboard-program-board widget-configurable" ${widgetAttrs("programs")}><div class="card-head"><div><span class="card-icon ci-coral">♧</span><strong>오늘의 프로그램</strong></div><div class="head-actions"><button data-program-sheet>시트 연결</button><button data-program-add>＋ 등록</button><button data-widget-settings="programs">⚙</button></div></div><div class="dashboard-program-columns"><section><header><div><small>SELECTED PROGRAM</small><h3>공통·선택프로그램</h3></div><span>${selection.length}건</span></header><div>${selection.length?selection.map(p=>dashboardProgramItem(p,"selection")).join(""):'<div class="program-empty compact">오늘 진행되는 공통·선택프로그램이 없습니다.</div>'}</div></section><section><header><div><small>AFTER SCHOOL</small><h3>방과후·야간심화</h3></div><span>${after.length}건</span></header><div>${after.length?after.map(p=>dashboardProgramItem(p,"after")).join(""):'<div class="program-empty compact">오늘 진행되는 방과후·야간심화수업이 없습니다.</div>'}</div></section></div><div class="widget-source-footer program-source">출처: 선택활동 DB · 방과후 수업 · 야간심화 · 41_학교캘린더</div></article>`;
}

function isWholeSchoolCommonActivity(program){
  if(!program)return false;
  const type=String(program.type||program.category||program.programType||program.activityType||'');
  const target=String(program.target||program.targetText||program.audience||program.participantScope||'');
  const common=type.includes('공통')||type.includes('공통활동');
  const whole=target.includes('전교생')||target.includes('전체')||target.includes('1학년 전체')||target.includes('공통');
  return common&&whole;
}
function isWholeSchoolDashboardCalendarEvent(event){
  if(!event)return false;
  const target=String(event.target||event.targetText||event.audience||'').replace(/\s/g,'');
  const type=String(event.type||event.category||'');
  const title=String(event.title||'');
  const whole=/전교생|전체학생|전학년|학생전체|학교전체/.test(target);
  const schoolWideCeremony=/개학식|방학식|입학식|졸업식/.test(title)&&(!target||whole);
  return whole||(/공통행사/.test(type)&&whole)||schoolWideCeremony;
}

  const directClass=Number(row?.classNo??row?.class??row?.반??0);

  const raw=String(row?.classCode??row?.classroom??row?.학급??row?.반코드??row?.['학년반']??'').trim();
  const digits=raw.replace(/\D/g,'');
  if(digits.length===3){
    const grade=Number(digits[0

    // 1학년 학생 발굴은 운호고 실제 합격 입결을 중심축으로 사용합니다.
    // 수능최저DB가 없거나 전형명이 미래 기준과 정확히 일치하지 않아도 입결 사례를 버리지 않습니다.
    // 다만 해당 대학에 수능최저 규칙이 존재하고 현재 성적으로 명확히 미충족인 경우에는 배지를 만들지 않습니다.
    const checks=universityRules.map(rule=>({rule,check:minimumCheck(rule,mockRows)}));
    const anyMet=checks.find(x=>x.check.result==='충족'||x.check.result==='미적용');
    const allDefiniteFail=checks.length>0&&checks.every(x=>x.check.result==='미충족');
    if(allDefiniteFail)continue;
    const fallback=anyMet||checks.find(x=>x.check.result==='판단보류'||x.check.result==='자료부족')||null;

      pairReason:compatible.length?'2028 전형 큰 범주 연결':'운호고 실제 합격사례 기반 발굴',pairLevel:compatible[0]?.match?.level||1,
      discoveryReason:cut.discoveryReason||'',recheck:cut.recheck||'2029 실제 지원 시 최종 모집요강 재검증',discoveryMode:'입결우선 발굴'
    });
  }
  const best=new Map();
  evidence.forEach(item=>{const key=universityNameKey(item.university),prev=best.get(key);if(!prev||item.pairLevel>prev.pairLevel||(item.pairLevel===prev.pairLevel&&item.limit<prev.limit))best.set(key,item);});
  const result=[...best.values()].filter(item=>universityBadgeVisible(item.university)).sort((a,b)=>universityPriorityIndex(a.university)-universityPriorityIndex(b.university)||String(a.university).localeCompare(String(b.university),'ko'));
  studentBadgeCache.set(cacheKey,result);return result;
}
function studentUniversityBadges(student){
  return studentUniversityBadgeEvidence(student).map(item=>item.university);
}
function studentUniversityEvidenceFor(student,university){
  const key=universityNameKey(university);
  return studentUniversityBadgeEvidence(student).find(item=>universityNameKey(item.university)===key)||null;
}
function studentAchievementBadgesMarkup(student){
  const universities=studentUniversityBadges(student);
  const allOne=studentAllOneGrade(student);
  const dorm=studentDormBadge(student);
  if(!allOne&&!universities.length&&!dorm)return '';
  const shown=universities.slice(0,4);
  return `<div class="student-achievement-badges no-print">${allOne?`<button type="button" class="achievement-badge all-one" data-student-badge-detail="all-one" data-badge-student-id="${escapeHtml(student.id||'')}">ALL 1 · 내신</button>`:''}${dorm?`<button type="button" class="achievement-badge dormitory" data-student-badge-detail="dorm" data-badge-student-id="${escapeHtml(student.id||'')}">${escapeHtml(dorm.label)}</button>`:''}${shown.map(name=>`<button type="button" class="achievement-badge university" data-student-badge-detail="university" data-badge-university="${escapeHtml(name)}" data-badge-student-id="${escapeHtml(student.id||'')}">${escapeHtml(name)} ✓</button>`).join('')}${universities.length>shown.length?`<button type="button" class="achievement-badge more" data-university-badge-list="${escapeHtml(student.id||'')}">+${universities.length-shown.length}</button>`:''}</div>`;
}
function studentPrintBadgesMarkup(student){
  const allOne=studentAllOneGrade(student), dorm=studentDormBadge(student), universities=studentUniversityBadges(student);
  const stages=studentVisibleAdmissionRows(student).filter(row=>row.stage==='core').map(row=>`${row.group==='inseoul'?'인서울':'지거국'} 핵심관리`);
  const items=[];
  if(allOne)items.push(['ALL 1 · 내신','score']);
  stages.forEach(x=>items.push([x,'manage']));
  if(dorm)items.push([dorm.label,'dorm']);
  universities.slice(0,4).forEach(x=>items.push([`${x} ✓`,'univ']));
  if(universities.length>4)items.push([`외 ${universities.length-4}개 대학`,'univ']);
  return items.length?`<div class="student-print-badges">${items.map(([label,kind])=>`<span class="${kind}">${escapeHtml(label)}</span>`).join('')}</div>`:'';
}

function studentStatMetric(student){
  const matches=row=>studentMatches(row,student);
  // 야자는 참여율이 아니라 실제 참여 횟수를 TOP 선정의 1차 기준으로 사용합니다.
  const nightRows=(readonlyCache?.nightAttendance||readonlyCache?.attendance||[]).filter(matches);
  const nightSeen=new Set(),nightAttendSeen=new Set();
  nightRows.forEach(row=>{
    const key=[row.date||row.day||'',row.time||row.slot||row.period||row.type||'',row.programId||row.programName||''].join('|');
    nightSeen.add(key);
    const attended=typeof nightRecordIsAttendance==='function'?nightRecordIsAttendance(row):/출석|정상|입실|참여/.test(String(row.result||row.status||''));
    if(attended)nightAttendSeen.add(key);
  });
  const nightTotal=nightSeen.size,nightAttend=nightAttendSeen.size;

  // 방과후는 학생별 분모(신청 차시)가 달라지는 비율 대신 실제 수강한 차시 수를 집계합니다.
  const afterSessions=typeof afterSchoolProgramGroups==='function'?afterSchoolProgramGroups():[];
  const afterSeen=new Set(),afterAttendSeen=new Set(),afterCourseKeys=new Set();
  afterSessions.filter(p=>p&&p.kind==='after'&&!p.isCourseMaster).forEach(program=>{
    const participant=(program.students||[]).find(matches);
    if(!participant)return;
    const sessionKey=String(program.sessionId||program.id||`${program.date||''}|${program.actualTitle||program.title||''}|${program.time||''}`);
    if(afterSeen.has(sessionKey))return;
    afterSeen.add(sessionKey);
    const status=typeof resolvedAfterAttendanceStatus==='function'?resolvedAfterAttendanceStatus(program,participant):normalizeAfterAttendanceStatus(participant.attendance||participant.result||participant.participation);
    const attended=status==='수강'||(typeof nightRecordIsAttendance==='function'&&nightRecordIsAttendance(participant));
    if(attended)afterAttendSeen.add(sessionKey);
    const courseKey=String(program.courseId||program.programId||program.actualTitle||program.title||'');
    if(courseKey)afterCourseKeys.add(courseKey);
  });
  const afterTotal=afterSeen.size,afterAttend=afterAttendSeen.size,afterCourseCount=afterCourseKeys.size;

  // 프로그램 참여는 전체 프로그램을 분모로 둔 비율이 아니라 실제 선택활동 참여 프로그램 수를 사용합니다.
  const bundle=typeof studentRecordBundle==='function'?studentRecordBundle(student):{programs:[]};
  const selectedProgramKeys=new Set((bundle.programs||[]).filter(p=>p&&p.kind==='selected'&&!/취소|폐강/.test(String(p.status||''))).map(p=>String(p.programId||p.id||programStableKey(p))));
  const programCount=selectedProgramKeys.size;
  const reports=studentCanonicalReports(student).length;
  const universities=studentUniversityBadges(student);
  return {
    student,
    nightAttend,nightTotal,nightRate:nightTotal?Math.round(nightAttend/nightTotal*100):0,
    afterAttend,afterTotal,afterCourseCount,afterRate:afterTotal?Math.round(afterAttend/afterTotal*100):0,
    programCount,reports,
    universityCount:universities.length,universities,allOne:studentAllOneGrade(student)
  };
}
function studentStatsRankCard(title,subtitle,metric,rows,valueFn,metaFn){
  const top=rows.slice(0,10);
  return `<section class="student-stat-rank-card"><div class="student-stat-card-head"><div><h3>${escapeHtml(title)}</h3><p>${escapeHtml(subtitle)}</p></div></div><div class="student-stat-rank-list">${top.length?top.map((row,i)=>`<button type="button" class="student-stat-rank-row" data-stat-student="${escapeHtml(row.student.id||'')}" data-stat-metric="${metric}"><em>${i+1}</em><span><b>${escapeHtml(row.student.studentNo||'')} ${escapeHtml(row.student.name||'')}</b><small>${escapeHtml(classNumberOf(row.student)||'-')}반 · ${escapeHtml(metaFn(row))}</small></span><strong>${escapeHtml(valueFn(row))}</strong></button>`).join(''):'<div class="query-empty"><b>집계 자료가 없습니다.</b></div>'}</div></section>`;
}
function studentGrowthMetric(row){
  const student=row.student, scores=uepRowsForStudent(uepStudentPerfIndex().scoreByStudent,String(student.id||''));
  const examOrder=['3월모의고사','6월모의고사','9월모의고사','10월모의고사','11월모의고사','수능'];
  const exams=examOrder.map(exam=>{const vals=scores.filter(x=>x.scoreType==='모의고사'&&x.exam===exam).map(x=>Number(x.level)).filter(Number.isFinite);return vals.length?{exam,avg:vals.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/vals.length}:null;}).filter(Boolean);
  const growth=exams.length>=2?Math.max(0,exams[0].avg-exams[exams.length-1].avg):0;
  const bundle=studentRecordBundle(student), activityTypes=new Set();
  if(row.nightAttend)activityTypes.add('야자'); if(row.afterAttend)activityTypes.add('방과후'); if(row.programCount)activityTypes.add('프로그램'); if(row.reports)activityTypes.add('보고서'); if(row.universityCount)activityTypes.add('대학배지');
  (bundle.activities||[]).forEach(a=>{const t=String(a.area||a.type||a.title||'').trim();if(t)activityTypes.add(t.slice(0,12));});
  const diversity=activityTypes.size;
  const hidden=Math.round(growth*30 + Math.min(30,row.programCount*4) + Math.min(25,row.reports*3) + Math.min(15,diversity*2));
  return {...row,growth,diversity,hidden,growthReason:exams.length>=2?`${exams[0].exam} 평균 ${exams[0].avg.toFixed(2)} → ${exams[exams.length-1].exam} ${exams[exams.length-1].avg.toFixed(2)}`:'비교 가능한 모의고사 2회 미만',diversityReason:`활동 ${diversity}개 영역 · 프로그램 ${row.programCount} · 보고서 ${row.reports}`,hiddenReason:`성적상승 ${growth.toFixed(2)} · 프로그램 ${row.programCount} · 보고서 ${row.reports} · 활동영역 ${diversity}`};
}

function routeStudentStatDetail(student,metric){
  if(!student)return;
  selectedStudentId=student.id||student.studentNo||"";
  const index=(readonlyCache?.students||[]).findIndex(s=>String(s.id||s.studentNo||"")===String(student.id||student.studentNo||""));
  // 학생 발굴·통계의 ‘해당 페이지로 이동’은 지표의 실제 원본 화면으로 정확히 연결합니다.
  if(metric==='night'){
    startStudentFocus(student,{originScroll:window.scrollY,originPage:'student-stats'});
    attendanceMode='night';
    nightClassFilter=String(classNumberOf(student)||'1');
    state.activePage='attendance';
    render('attendance');
  }else if(metric==='after'){
    startStudentFocus(student,{originScroll:window.scrollY,originPage:'student-stats'});
    programMode='after';
    if(typeof programViewMode!=="undefined")programViewMode='list';
    state.activePage='programs';
    render('programs');
  }else if(metric==='program'||metric==='report'){
    startStudentFocus(student,{originScroll:window.scrollY,originPage:'student-stats'});
    if(index>=0)studentDashboardIndex=index;
    studentDashboardSubmode='participation';
    studentPageMode='dashboard';
    state.activePage='students';
    render('students');
  }else if(metric==='university'){
    startStudentFocus(student,{originScroll:window.scrollY,originPage:'student-stats'});
    admissionMode='combined';
    state.activePage='admissions';
    render('admissions');
    requestAnimationFrame(()=>applyStudentFocusToPage('admissions'));
  }else{
    studentFocusContext=null;
    if(index>=0)studentDashboardIndex=index;
    studentDashboardSubmode='dashboard';
    studentPageMode='dashboard';
    state.activePage='students';
    render('students');
  }
  $('#drawer')?.classList.add('hidden');$('#drawerBackdrop')?.classList.add('hidden');
  window.scrollTo(0,0);
}
function openStudentStatDetail(studentId,metric){
  const student=(readonlyCache?.students||[]).find(s=>String(s.id||'')===String(studentId||''));if(!student)return;
  const matches=row=>studentMatches(row,student)&&studentRecordWithinEnrollment(row,student);let title='',rows=[],route='students';
  if(metric==='night'){title='야자 참여 이력';route='attendance-night';rows=(readonlyCache?.nightAttendance||readonlyCache?.attendance||[]).filter(matches).filter(r=>typeof nightRecordIsAttendance==='function'?nightRecordIsAttendance(r):/출석|정상|입실|참여/.test(String(r.result||r.status||''))).map(r=>[r.date||r.day||'-',r.time||r.slot||r.period||'야자',r.result||r.status||'출석']);}
  else if(metric==='after'){title='방과후 참여 이력';route='programs-after';rows=(typeof afterSchoolProgramGroups==='function'?afterSchoolProgramGroups():[]).filter(p=>p&&p.kind==='after'&&!p.isCourseMaster&&studentRecordWithinEnrollment(p,student)&&(p.students||[]).some(x=>studentMatches(x,student))).map(p=>[p.date||'-',p.actualTitle||p.title||'방과후',p.time||p.session||'수강']);}
  else if(metric==='program'){title='프로그램 참여 이력';route='student-participation';const b=studentRecordBundle(student);rows=(b.programs||[]).filter(p=>p.kind==='selected'&&!/취소|폐강/.test(String(p.status||''))).map(p=>[p.date||'-',p.actualTitle||p.title||'선택활동',p.status||'참여']);}
  else if(metric==='report'){title='제출 보고서';route='student-participation';rows=studentCanonicalReports(student).map(r=>[r.date||r.submittedAt||'-',r.programTitle||r.programName||r.reportGroup||r.activityTitle||r.title||'보고서',r.status||'제출']);}
  else if(metric==='university'){title='대학 배지';route='admissions-combined';rows=studentUniversityBadges(student).map(name=>[name,'대학입결 ✓','수능최저 ✓']);}
  else {const m=studentGrowthMetric(studentStatMetric(student)); title=metric==='growth'?'성적 상승 선정 근거':metric==='diversity'?'활동 다양성 선정 근거':'숨은 성장학생 선정 근거'; route='students'; const reason=metric==='growth'?m.growthReason:metric==='diversity'?m.diversityReason:m.hiddenReason; rows=[[title,reason,metric==='growth'?`상승 ${m.growth.toFixed(2)}`:metric==='diversity'?`${m.diversity}영역`:`${m.hidden}점`]];}
  $('#drawerTitle').textContent=`${student.name} · ${title}`;

function openWidgetSettings(key) {

    width: "auto",
    fontScale: 100,
  };

  $("#drawerTitle").textContent = `${labels[key] || key} 위젯 설정`;
  $("#drawerBody").innerHTML =
    `<form id="widgetSettingsForm" class="widget-settings-form"><div class="widget-setting-grid"><label>표시 여부<select id="widgetVisible"><option value="true" ${setting.visible !== false ? "selected" : ""}>표시</option><option value="false" ${setting.visible === false ? "selected" : ""}>숨김</option></select></label><label>너비<select id="widgetWidth"><option value="auto" ${setting.width === "auto" ? "selected" : ""}>자동</option><option value="wide" ${setting.width === "wide" ? "selected" : ""}>넓게</option><option value="fixed" ${setting.width === "fixed" ? "selected" : ""}>고정</option></select></label><label>글자 크기<div class="font-stepper"><button type="button" data-font-minus>−</button><output id="widgetFontScale">${setting.fontScale || 100}%</output><button type="button" data-font-plus>＋</button></div></label></div>${key === "timetable" ? `<div class="alert-settings"><label><input type="checkbox" id="showPeriodTimes" ${setting.showTimes !== false ? "checked" : ""}> 교시별 시간 표시</label><label><input type="checkbox" id="startPeriodAlert" ${setting.startAlert ? "checked" : ""}> 수업 시작 알림</label><label><input type="checkbox" id="endPeriodAlert" ${setting.endAlert ? "checked" : ""}> 수업 종료 알림</label></div>` : ""}<button class="btn primary">로컬 설정 저장</button><p class="drawer-note">위젯 설정은 이 PC에만 저장됩니다. 데이터 원본은 수정하지 않습니다.</p></form>`;
  $("#drawer").classList.remove("hidden");
  $("#drawerBackdrop").classList.remove("hidden");
  let scale = setting.fontScale || 100;
  const updateScale = () => ($("#widgetFontScale").textContent = `${scale}%`);
  $("[data-font-minus]").onclick = () => {
    scale = Math.max(80, scale - 10);
    updateScale();
  };
  $("[data-font-plus]").onclick = () => {
    scale = Math.min(130, scale + 10);
    updateScale();
  };
  $("#widgetSettingsForm").onsubmit = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    state.settings.widgetSettings[key] = {
      ...setting,
      visible: $("#widgetVisible").value === "true",
      width: $("#widgetWidth").value,
      fontScale: scale,
      ...(key === "timetable"
        ? {
            showTimes: $("#showPeriodTimes").checked,
            startAlert: $("#startPeriodAlert").checked,
            endAlert: $("#endPeriodAlert").checked,
          }
        : {}),
    };
    if (key === "timetable" && (state.settings.widgetSettings[key].startAlert || state.settings.widgetSettings[key].endAlert)) {
      try { if ("Notification" in window && Notification.permission === "default") await Notification.requestPermission(); } catch {}
    }
    await save();
    closeDrawer();
    render(state.activePage);
    toast("위젯 설정을 저장했습니다.");
  };
}


function bindProgramRosterGradeFilter() {
  const rows = [...document.querySelectorAll('[data-program-student-grade]')];
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-program-grade]').forEach(button=>{
    button.onclick=()=>{
      const grade=button.dataset.programGrade||'all';
      document.querySelectorAll('[data-program-grade]').forEach(item=>item.classList.toggle('active',item===button));
      rows.forEach(row=>row.classList.toggle('hidden',grade!=='all'&&row.dataset.programStudentGrade!==grade));
      const visible=rows.filter(row=>!row.classList.contains('hidden')).length;
      const count=document.querySelector('#programRosterVisibleCount'); if(count) count.textContent=`${visible}명 표시`;
    };
  });
}

}

function normalizeAfterAttendanceStatus(value){
  const text=String(value||"").trim();
  if(/불참\(인정\)|공결|공식 ?행사|인정/.test(text))return "불참(인정)";
  if(/불참\(조퇴\)|조퇴/.test(text))return "불참(조퇴)";
  if(/불참\(결석\)|병결|결석/.test(text))return "불참(결석)";
  if(/불참\(미인정\)|미인정|무단|불참/.test(text))return "불참(미인정)";
  if(/수강|출석|참여|정상|입실|지각/.test(text))return "수강";
  return "신청";
}
function afterAttendanceOverrideKey(program,student){
  return [program.sessionId||program.id||"",student.studentId||student.studentNo||student.number||student.name||""].join("|");
}
function resolvedAfterAttendanceStatus(program,student){
  const key=afterAttendanceOverrideKey(program,student);
  return state.afterAttendanceOverrides?.[key]||normalizeAfterAttendanceStatus(student.attendance||student.result||student.participation);
}

function normalizeAfterAttendanceStatus(value){
  const text=String(value||"").trim();
  if(/불참\(인정\)|공결|공식 ?행사|인정/.test(text))return "불참(인정)";
  if(/불참\(조퇴\)|조퇴/.test(text))return "불참(조퇴)";
  if(/불참\(결석\)|병결|결석/.test(text))return "불참(결석)";
  if(/불참\(미인정\)|미인정|무단|불참/.test(text))return "불참(미인정)";
  if(/수강|출석|참여|정상|입실|지각/.test(text))return "수강";
  return "신청";
}
function afterAttendanceOverrideKey(program,student){
  return [program.sessionId||program.id||"",student.studentId||student.studentNo||student.number||student.name||""].join("|");
}
function resolvedAfterAttendanceStatus(program,student){
  const key=afterAttendanceOverrideKey(program,student);
  return state.afterAttendanceOverrides?.[key]||normalizeAfterAttendanceStatus(student.attendance||student.result||student.participation);
}
const AFTER_ATTENDANCE_STATUSES=["신청","수강","불참(미인정)","불참(결석)","불참(조퇴)","불참(인정)"];

function openAfterschoolProgramDrawer(id) {

  const program = programWithOverride(sourceProgram);

  $("#drawerKicker").textContent = "AFTER-SCHOOL PROGRAM";

function bindProgramRosterGradeFilter() {
  const rows = [...document.querySelectorAll('[data-program-student-grade]')];
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-program-grade]').forEach(button=>{
    button.onclick=()=>{
      const grade=button.dataset.programGrade||'all';
      document.querySelectorAll('[data-program-grade]').forEach(item=>item.classList.toggle('active',item===button));
      rows.forEach(row=>row.classList.toggle('hidden',grade!=='all'&&row.dataset.programStudentGrade!==grade));
      const visible=rows.filter(row=>!row.classList.contains('hidden')).length;
      const count=document.querySelector('#programRosterVisibleCount'); if(count) count.textContent=`${visible}명 표시`;
    };
  });
}
function programRosterTabs(students) {
  const counts={1:0,2:0,3:0}; students.forEach(student=>{const grade=programStudentGrade(student);if(counts[grade]!=null)counts[grade]+=1;});
  return `<div class="program-roster-filter"><div><button class="active" data-program-grade="all">전체 ${students.length}명</button>${[1,2,3].map(grade=>`<button data-program-grade="${grade}" ${counts[grade]?"":"disabled"}>${grade}학년 ${counts[grade]}명</button>`).join("")}</div><span id="programRosterVisibleCount">${students.length}명 표시</span></div>`;
}

  $("#drawerTitle").textContent=program.actualTitle||program.title||display.label;

function normalizeAfterAttendanceStatus(value){
  const text=String(value||"").trim();
  if(/불참\(인정\)|공결|공식 ?행사|인정/.test(text))return "불참(인정)";
  if(/불참\(조퇴\)|조퇴/.test(text))return "불참(조퇴)";
  if(/불참\(결석\)|병결|결석/.test(text))return "불참(결석)";
  if(/불참\(미인정\)|미인정|무단|불참/.test(text))return "불참(미인정)";
  if(/수강|출석|참여|정상|입실|지각/.test(text))return "수강";
  return "신청";
}
function afterAttendanceOverrideKey(program,student){
  return [program.sessionId||program.id||"",student.studentId||student.studentNo||student.number||student.name||""].join("|");
}
function resolvedAfterAttendanceStatus(program,student){
  const key=afterAttendanceOverrideKey(program,student);
  return state.afterAttendanceOverrides?.[key]||normalizeAfterAttendanceStatus(student.attendance||student.result||student.participation);
}
