function timetableSubjectStyle(subject) {
  const text=String(subject||"").replace(/\s+/g,"");
  const rules=[
    [/국어|한문/,"subject-korean"],[/수학/,"subject-math"],[/영어/,"subject-english"],
    [/사회|한국사|역사|지리|윤리/,"subject-social"],[/과학|물리|화학|생명|지구/,"subject-science"],
    [/진로|동아리|자율|창체/,"subject-creative"],[/체육|음악|미술/,"subject-art"]
  ];
  return rules.find(([pattern])=>pattern.test(text))?.[1]||"subject-other";
}

function shiftDateKey(key, delta) {
  /* UEP_08197_ATTENDANCE_DATE_SHIFT */
  const match = String(key || "").match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/);
  if (!match) return dateKey(today);
  const year = Number(match[1]), month = Number(match[2]), day = Number(match[3]);
  const date = new Date(year, month - 1, day + Number(delta || 0), 12, 0, 0, 0);
  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return dateKey(today);
  return date.getFullYear() + "-" + String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0") + "-" + String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, "0");
}
function attendanceDateControls(label,actionHtml="") {
  return `<div class="attendance-date-controls"><span>${escapeHtml(label)}</span><button class="btn secondary" data-attendance-date-step="-1">‹</button><div class="attendance-date-input-wrap"><input type="date" id="attendanceViewDate" value="${escapeHtml(attendanceViewDate)}"><strong>${escapeHtml(weekdayLabelFromKey(attendanceViewDate, true))}</strong></div><button class="btn secondary" data-attendance-date-step="1">›</button><button class="btn secondary" data-attendance-today>오늘</button>${actionHtml?`<div class="attendance-date-action">${actionHtml}</div>`:""}</div>`;
}
function nightSlotName(rowOrText) {
  const text = typeof rowOrText === "string" ? rowOrText : `${rowOrText?.time || ""} ${rowOrText?.type || ""} ${rowOrText?.detail || ""}`;
  // 운호고 방과후 8교시는 오후자습 시간대이다.
  if (/오후자습|1타임|8교시/.test(text)) return "오후자습";
  if (/야자\s*1|야간\s*1|2타임/.test(text)) return "야자1";
  if (/야자\s*2|야간\s*2|3타임/.test(text)) return "야자2";
  return String(rowOrText?.time || rowOrText?.type || text || "기타").trim();
}
function minutesOfClock(value) {
  const match = String(value || "").match(/(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/);
  return match ? Number(match[1]) * 60 + Number(match[2]) : null;
}
function slotRange(slot) {
  return {"오후자습":[17*60,18*60],"야자1":[19*60+20,20*60+10],"야자2":[20*60+20,21*60+30]}[slot] || null;
}
function isWednesdayKey(value) {
  const key=String(value||"").slice(0,10);
  if(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(key)){
    const d=new Date(`${key}T12:00:00`);
    return !Number.isNaN(d.getTime())&&d.getDay()===3;
  }
  return /^수(?:요일)?$/.test(String(value||"").trim());
}
function programHasNightStudyImpact(program) {
  const dates=[program?.date,...(Array.isArray(program?.dates)?program.dates:[])].filter(Boolean);
  if(dates.length&&dates.every(isWednesdayKey)) return false;
  const weekdayText=String(program?.weekdays||program?.weekday||program?.day||"").replace(/\s/g,"");
  if(weekdayText&&/^(수|수요일)$/.test(weekdayText)) return false;
  // UEP_08177_AFTER_SCHOOL_NIGHT_LINK_ONLY: 문자열 N을 Boolean(N)으로 처리하지 않는다.
  const raw=program?.affectsAttendance??program?.nightLinked??program?.nightLink??program?.['야자연계여부'];
  if(raw===true)return true;
  if(raw===false||raw==null)return false;
  return /^(Y|YES|TRUE|1|예|연계|적용)$/i.test(String(raw).trim());
}
function singlePeriodLabel(session) {
  const candidates=[session?.period,session?.startTime,session?.time].map(v=>String(v||"").trim()).filter(Boolean);
  for(const text of candidates){
    const m=text.match(/(\d+)교시/);
    if(m) return `${m[1]}교시`;
  }
  return String(session?.time||session?.period||"시간 확인");
}
function programOverlapsSlot(program, slot) {
  if(isWednesdayKey(program?.date)||!programHasNightStudyImpact(program)) return false;
  const descriptor = `${program?.time || ""} ${program?.title || ""} ${program?.actualTitle || ""} ${program?.type || ""}`;
  // 차시 시작시각이 8교시라면 시트의 기존 야자영향타임 값보다 학교 운영 기준을 우선한다.
  // 즉 8교시는 야자1이 아니라 오후자습으로만 표시한다.
  if (/8교시/.test(descriptor)) return slot === "오후자습";
  const explicitSlots=(program?.nightSlots||[]).flatMap(value=>{
    const text=String(value||"");
    return ["오후자습","야자1","야자2"].filter(name=>text.includes(name));
  });
  if(explicitSlots.length) return explicitSlots.includes(slot);
  const range = slotRange(slot);
  const namedSlot = nightSlotName(descriptor);
  if (namedSlot && ["오후자습","야자1","야자2"].includes(namedSlot) && !String(namedSlot).includes("기타")) return namedSlot === slot;
  const clocks = descriptor.match(/\d{1,2}:\d{2}/g) || [];
  if (!range || clocks.length < 2) return Boolean(program?.affectsAttendance && /오후자습/.test(descriptor) && slot === "오후자습");
  const start = minutesOfClock(clocks[0]), end = minutesOfClock(clocks[1]);
  return Number.isFinite(start) && Number.isFinite(end) && start < range[1] && end > range[0];
}
function programParticipationFor(student, day, slot) {
  const id = String(student?.id || student?.studentId || "");
  const number = String(student?.studentNo || "");
  const connected = (readonlyCache?.programs || []).filter(program=>program?.kind!=="after");
  const derived = typeof afterSchoolProgramGroups === "function" ? afterSchoolProgramGroups() : [];
  const seen = new Set();
  const programs = [...connected, ...derived].filter(program => {
    const key = `${program.id || ""}|${program.date || ""}|${program.title || ""}|${program.time || ""}`;
    if (seen.has(key)) return false; seen.add(key); return true;
  });
  for (const program of programs) {
    if(isWholeSchoolCommonActivity(program))continue;
    if (program.date !== day || !programOverlapsSlot(program, slot)) continue;
    const target = (program.students || []).find(item => String(item.studentId || "") === id || (number && String(item.studentNo || item.number || "") === number));
    if (!target) continue;
    const participationText = [target.result,target.attendance,target.participation,target.status,target.checkMethod].filter(Boolean).join(" ");
    const inactive = /결석|불참|취소|중도포기|퇴강|미참여/.test(participationText);
    // 13_출석부의 활성 수강명단은 출석상태가 '등록'이어도 해당 실제 차시에는 프로그램 참여자로 본다.
    // 실제 수업일·야자영향타임은 program.date/program.time 단계에서 이미 검증되므로 명단 등록만으로 다른 날짜에 표시되지 않는다.
    const confirmed = !inactive && ((typeof nightRecordIsAttendance === "function" && nightRecordIsAttendance(target)) || /출석|정상|참여|입실|명단QR|학사|등록|신청/.test(participationText));
    return {program, target, confirmed, pending: !confirmed && !inactive};
  }
  return null;
}
function nightProgramStatus(program){
  const text=`${program?.afterType||""} ${program?.type||""} ${program?.actualTitle||program?.title||""}`;
  if(/야간심화|심화수업/.test(text)) return {cls:"after",label:"야간심화",kind:"after"};
  if(/일반 방과후|방학 방과후|방과후학교/.test(text)) return {cls:"after",label:"방과후",kind:"after"};
  const title=String(program?.actualTitle||program?.title||"프로그램");
  return {cls:"program",label:title.length>8?`${title.slice(0,7)}…`:title,kind:program?.kind||"general"};
}
function attendanceIsWeekday(day){const n=new Date(`${day}T12:00:00`).getDay();return n>=1&&n<=5;}
function attendanceIsSchoolHoliday(day){
  const key=String(day||'').slice(0,10);
  const fixed2026=new Set(['2026-01-01','2026-02-16','2026-02-17','2026-02-18','2026-03-01','2026-03-02','2026-05-05','2026-05-24','2026-05-25','2026-06-06','2026-08-15','2026-08-17','2026-09-24','2026-09-25','2026-09-26','2026-10-03','2026-10-05','2026-10-09','2026-12-25']);
  if(fixed2026.has(key))return true;
  const closureRx=/공휴일|대체공휴일|재량휴업|휴업일|개교기념일|임시공휴일|석가탄신일|부처님오신날|어린이날|현충일|광복절|개천절|한글날|성탄절|설날|추석/;
  return schoolEventsForDate(key).some(event=>{
    const title=String(event.title||'').trim(); const type=String(event.type||'').trim();
    if(!closureRx.test(title+' '+type))return false;
    if(/공휴일|대체공휴일|재량휴업|휴업일|개교기념일|임시공휴일/.test(title+' '+type))return true;
    const start=String(event.date||'').slice(0,10), end=String(event.endDate||event.date||'').slice(0,10);
    return start===key&&end===key;
  });
}
function isNightOperatingDay(day){return attendanceIsWeekday(day)&&!attendanceIsSchoolHoliday(day);}
function nearestNightOperatingDay(day){
  let d=new Date(`${day}T12:00:00`);
  for(let i=0;i<31;i++){const key=dateKey(d);if(isNightOperatingDay(key))return key;d.setDate(d.getDate()-1);}
  return day;
}
function shiftNightOperatingDate(day,direction){
  let d=new Date(`${day}T12:00:00`);
  const step=Number(direction)>=0?1:-1;
  for(let i=0;i<62;i++){
    d.setDate(d.getDate()+step);
    const key=dateKey(d);
    if(isNightOperatingDay(key))return key;
  }
  return day;
}

function dashboardPersonalWeekMarkup(){
  const {week,days}=personalTimetableData();
  const status=schoolPeriodStatus(new Date());
  const todayIndex=Math.max(0,Math.min(4,(today.getDay()+6)%7));
  const maxPeriods=Math.max(7,Array.isArray(week)?week.length:0);
  if(!Array.isArray(week)||!week.some(row=>Array.isArray(row)&&row.some(Boolean))){
    return `<div class="uep-week-timetable-empty"><span>◷</span><b>내 주간시간표 연결 준비</b><small>컴시간 교사시간표가 연결되면 월~금 수업이 이곳에 표시됩니다.</small><button data-page="timetable">시간표 연결 확인</button></div>`;
  }
  const heads=(days?.length?days:["월","화","수","목","금"]).slice(0,5);
  const cells=[];
  for(let period=1;period<=maxPeriods;period+=1){
    cells.push(`<div class="uep-week-period"><b>${period}</b><small>${escapeHtml(periodTimeLabel(period)||"")}</small></div>`);
    for(let d=0;d<5;d+=1){
      const raw=week[period-1]?.[d]||"";
      const [subject,room,flag]=String(raw).split("|");
      const current=d===todayIndex&&status.index===period-1;
      const classColor=timetableClassColorClass(room);
      cells.push(`<button class="uep-week-class personal-order ${raw?"has-class":"is-empty"} ${classColor} ${current?"is-current":""} ${flag==="changed"?"is-changed":""}" data-page="timetable"><b>${escapeHtml(room||"-")}</b><small>${escapeHtml(subject||"")}</small>${current?'<i aria-label="현재 교시"></i>':''}${flag==="changed"?'<em>변동</em>':''}</button>`);
    }
  }
  return `<div class="uep-week-timetable"><div class="uep-week-corner">교시</div>${heads.map((day,i)=>`<div class="uep-week-head ${i===todayIndex?"today":""}"><b>${escapeHtml(String(day).replace(/요일/g,""))}</b></div>`).join("")}${cells.join("")}</div>`;
}

function currentTeacherAccessScope(){
  const role=currentRoleId();
  const teacher=currentLoginTeacherName();
  const profile=currentUserProfile();
  const defaultGrade=String(profile.grade||state?.settings?.grade||state?.settings?.neis?.grade||1);
  const homeroomRow=(SOURCE_REFERENCE_DATA?.homeroom||[]).find(row=>String(row?.[2]||'').trim()===teacher);
  const grade=String(profile.grade||homeroomRow?.[0]||defaultGrade);
  const classNo=String(profile.classNo||homeroomRow?.[1]||state?.settings?.neis?.classNo||'');
  if(role==='grade_head'||role==='grade_manager') return {role,teacher,grade,classNo:'',label:`${grade}학년 전체 조회`};
  if(role==='admin'||role==='vice_principal'||role==='principal') return {role,teacher,grade:'',classNo:'',label:'학교 전체 조회'};
  if(role==='homeroom') return {role,teacher,grade,classNo,label:classNo?`${grade}학년 전체 조회 · ${classNo}반 업무수정`:`${grade}학년 전체 조회`};
  return {role,teacher,grade,classNo,label:`${grade}학년 조회`};
}
function recordGradeClass(row){
  const studentNo=String(row?.studentNo||row?.number||row?.studentNumber||'').replace(/\D/g,'');
  const grade=String(row?.grade||row?.gradeNo||row?.studentGrade||(studentNo.length>=4?studentNo[0]:'')||'');
  let classNo=String(row?.classNo||row?.className||row?.class||'').replace(/[^0-9]/g,'');
  if(!classNo&&studentNo.length>=4) classNo=String(Number(studentNo.slice(1,3))||'');
  return {grade,classNo};
}
function filterRowsByCurrentTeacherScope(rows){
  const scope=currentTeacherAccessScope();
  if(!Array.isArray(rows))return [];
  if(!scope.grade&&!scope.classNo)return rows.slice();
  return rows.filter(row=>{
    const gc=recordGradeClass(row);
    if(scope.grade&&gc.grade&&gc.grade!==scope.grade)return false;
    if(scope.classNo&&gc.classNo&&String(Number(gc.classNo))!==String(Number(scope.classNo)))return false;
    return true;
  });
}
function dashboardGradeScope(){
  const profile=currentUserProfile();
  const scope=currentTeacherAccessScope();
  const grade=String(profile.grade||scope.grade||state?.settings?.neis?.grade||1);
  return {grade,label:`${grade}학년 전체`};
}
function filterRowsForDashboardStatus(rows){
  const scope=dashboardGradeScope();
  if(!Array.isArray(rows))return [];
  return rows.filter(row=>{
    const gc=recordGradeClass(row);
    return !scope.grade||!gc.grade||String(gc.grade)===String(scope.grade);
  });
}
function dashboardOwnClassNo(){
  const profile=currentUserProfile();
  if(profile.classNo)return String(Number(profile.classNo));
  const teacher=currentLoginTeacherName();
  const row=(SOURCE_REFERENCE_DATA?.homeroom||[]).find(item=>String(item?.[2]||'').trim()===teacher);
  return row?.[1]?String(Number(row[1])):'';
}
function resolveDashboardStudent(row){
  const students=Array.isArray(readonlyCache?.students)?readonlyCache.students:[];
  const ids=[row?.studentId,row?.studentID,row?.id,row?.studentNo,row?.studentNumber].map(value=>String(value||'').trim()).filter(Boolean);
  const name=String(row?.name||row?.studentName||row?.displayName||'').trim();
  const gc=recordGradeClass(row);
  return students.find(student=>{
    const studentIds=[student?.id,student?.studentId,student?.studentID,student?.studentNo,student?.studentNumber].map(value=>String(value||'').trim()).filter(Boolean);
    if(ids.some(id=>studentIds.includes(id)))return true;
    if(name&&name===String(student?.name||student?.studentName||student?.displayName||'').trim()){
      const studentGc=recordGradeClass(student);
      return (!gc.grade||!studentGc.grade||gc.grade===studentGc.grade)&&(!gc.classNo||!studentGc.classNo||String(Number(gc.classNo))===String(Number(studentGc.classNo)));
    }
    return false;
  })||null;
}

}
function openWorkAlertsDrawer(){
  const alerts=dashboardWorkAlerts();
  $("#drawerKicker").textContent="WORK ALERTS";$("#drawerTitle").textContent="업무 알림";
  $("#drawerBody").innerHTML=`<div class="work-alert-drawer"><header><b>${alerts.length}건 확인 필요</b><small>현재 사용자 · ${escapeHtml(currentWorkAudienceContext().name)} · ${escapeHtml(currentWorkAudienceContext().department)}</small></header>${alerts.length?alerts.map(({item,meta,priority})=>`<article class="level-${priority}"><button data-alert-notice-detail="${escapeHtml(item.id||"")}"><i>공지</i><span><b>${escapeHtml(item.title||"")}</b><small>${escapeHtml(meta)}</small></span><em>›</em></button></article>`).join(""):`<div class="work-board-empty"><b>확인할 알림이 없습니다.</b><small>새 학교업무 공지가 생기면 이곳에 표시됩니다.</small></div>`}</div>`;
  $("#drawer").classList.remove("hidden");$("#drawerBackdrop").classList.remove("hidden");
  $$("[data-alert-notice-detail]").forEach(button=>button.onclick=()=>openWorkNoticeDetail(button.dataset.alertNoticeDetail,"connected"));
}

function dashboardView() {
  const calendarMode=dashboardCalendarMode;
  const quick=[
    ["dorm-outing","학사외출현황","lookup","오늘 현황"],["night-supervisor","야자감독","lookup","현황 보기"],["meal-duty","급식지도","lookup","당번 보기"],
    ["approval","결재라인","lookup","확인하기"],["homeroom","담임·부담임","lookup","확인하기"],["extension","내선번호·자리배치","lookup","확인하기"],
    ["unho-llm","운호LLM","link","바로가기"],["portal","업무포털","link","바로가기"],["edufine","K-에듀파인","link","바로가기"],
    ["riro","리로스쿨","link","바로가기"],["training","충북교육연수","link","바로가기"],["school-site","운호고 홈페이지","link","바로가기"],
    ["comtime","컴시간","run","시간표 실행"],["messenger","충북소통메신저","run","메신저 실행"]
  ];
  const groups=[["lookup","팝업"],["link","링크"],["run","실행"]];
  const quickOrder=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('uepQuickOrder')||'{}');
  const quickOrderRank=Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(quickOrder).map(([kind,ids])=>[kind,Object.fromEntries((Array.isArray(ids)?ids:[]).map((id,index)=>[id,index]))]));
  quick.sort((a,b)=>{const aa=quickOrderRank[a[2]]?.[a[0]],bb=quickOrderRank[b[2]]?.[b[0]];return (Number.isInteger(aa)?aa:999)-(Number.isInteger(bb)?bb:999);});
  const connectionNotice=readonlyCache?.students?.length?"":`<div class="uep-connection-note"><span>!</span><div><b>학교 데이터 동기화를 확인하세요. 마지막 저장 자료는 계속 사용할 수 있습니다.</b><small>${escapeHtml(googleConnectionError||"서비스 계정과 공유 권한을 확인하세요.")}</small></div><button data-page="settings">설정</button></div>`;
  const calendarMarkup=calendarMode==='week'?dashboardWeekAgendaMarkup():dashboardMiniCalendarMarkup();
  return `<div class="uep-home uep-home-v0750 uep-home-v0755">
    <section class="uep-today-strip-v0755"><div class="uep-home-clock"><strong id="heroClock">오전 00:00:00</strong><span id="heroDate">날짜 확인 중</span></div><div class="uep-weather-inline"><i id="heroWeatherIcon">○</i><span><b id="heroWeatherText">청주 날씨</b><em id="heroWeatherTemp">--℃</em></span></div>${dashboardDutyBadgesMarkup()}</section>
    <header class="uep-home-header uep-home-header-v0755">
      <section class="uep-quick-area uep-quick-area-v0750 uep-quick-area-compact"><div class="uep-quick-groups">${groups.map(([kind,label])=>{const kindQuick=quick.filter(q=>q[2]===kind);return `<section class="uep-quick-group quick-${kind}"><b>${label} 메뉴 (${kindQuick.length})</b><div class="uep-quick-actions">${kindQuick.map(q=>`<button class="uep-quick-card-v0755" draggable="true" data-reference="${q[0]}" data-quick-kind="${q[2]}"><i class="uep-quick-icon"></i><span><b>${q[1]}</b></span></button>`).join("")}</div></section>`;}).join("")}</div></section>
    </header>${connectionNotice}
    <section class="uep-dashboard-priority-row uep-dashboard-three-line uep-dashboard-priority-v07631">
      <article class="uep-panel"><div class="uep-panel-head"><div><span class="peach">♧</span><strong>오늘의 프로그램</strong></div><button data-page="programs">전체보기</button></div>${dashboardCompactProgramsMarkup()}</article>
      <div class="uep-dashboard-work-row compact-work-card">${todayOperationsMarkup()}</div>
    </section>
    <section class="uep-dashboard-main-v0750 uep-dashboard-reference-row">
      <article class="uep-panel uep-panel-timetable-v0750"><div class="uep-panel-head timetable-panel-head"><div><span class="mint">◷</span><strong>내 주간시간표</strong></div>${dashboardTimetableHeaderControlsMarkup()}<nav class="dashboard-timetable-shortcuts"><button data-timetable-shortcut="homeroom">학급시간표</button><button data-timetable-shortcut="teacher">교사시간표</button><button data-school-hours-popup>일과운영시간</button></nav></div>${dashboardTimetablePanelMarkup()}</article>
      <article class="uep-panel uep-panel-calendar-v0750"><div class="uep-panel-head"><div><span class="peach">▣</span><strong>학교 캘린더</strong><nav class="uep-calendar-switch"><button class="${calendarMode==='month'?'active':''}" data-dashboard-calendar-mode="month">월간</button><button class="${calendarMode==='week'?'active':''}" data-dashboard-calendar-mode="week">주간</button></nav></div><button data-dashboard-calendar-full="${calendarMode}" data-dashboard-calendar-target="${dashboardSelectedDate}">전체보기</button></div>${calendarMarkup}</article>
    </section>
  </div>`;
}

function currentTeacherHomeroomClass(){
  // 0.81.42: 로그인 계정표의 담당학년·담임반을 최우선으로 사용합니다.
  const profile=currentUserProfile();
  const grade=String(profile.grade||'').replace(/\D/g,'');
  const classNo=String(profile.classNo||'').replace(/\D/g,'');
  if(grade&&classNo)return `${grade}-${classNo}`;
  const name=currentLoginTeacherName();
  const row=(SOURCE_REFERENCE_DATA?.homeroom||[]).find(item=>String(item?.[0])===String(grade||'1')&&String(item?.[2]||'').trim()===name);
  return row?`${normalizeTimetableGradeClass(row).grade}-${normalizeTimetableGradeClass(row).classNo}`:'';
}
function currentUserIsGradeHead(){
  const role=currentRoleId();
  if(role==='admin'||role==='grade_head'||role==='grade_manager')return true;
  const name=currentLoginTeacherName();
  return (DUTY_ASSIGNMENTS||[]).some(row=>String(row?.department||'').trim()==='1학년부'&&String(row?.role||'').trim()==='부장'&&String(row?.person||'').trim()===name);
}
function completionStatusMarkup(item){
  const required=(Array.isArray(item?.requiredClasses)&&item.requiredClasses.length?item.requiredClasses:['1-1','1-2','1-3','1-4','1-5','1-6','1-7','1-8','1-9']).map(String);
  const completed=(Array.isArray(item?.confirmedClasses)?item.confirmedClasses:Array.isArray(item?.completedClasses)?item.completedClasses:[]).map(String);
  if(currentUserIsGradeHead()){
    return `<div class="class-completion-mini"><b>${completed.length}/${required.length}반 완료</b><span>${required.map(cls=>`<i class="${completed.includes(cls)?'done':'pending'}">${escapeHtml(cls.replace('1-',''))}반</i>`).join('')}</span></div>`;
  }
  const mine=currentTeacherHomeroomClass();
  if(mine&&required.includes(mine))return `<em class="my-class-completion ${completed.includes(mine)?'done':'pending'}">우리 반 ${completed.includes(mine)?'완료':'미완료'}</em>`;
  return '';
}
function counselCandidateCompleted(candidate){
  const records=growthCounselRecords(candidate?.student?.id||'');
  return records.some(row=>String(row.status||'').trim()==='완료');
}
function counselPriorityCandidateForStudent(student){
  const base=admissionBase(student.id),mock=latestMockSnapshot(student.id),prep=studentPreparationStats(student);
  const actual9=base.actual9;
  const reasons=[],tags=[];
  let score=0;
  if(actual9!=null){
    const seoulRule=uepManagementRule('inseoul'),regionalRule=uepManagementRule('regional');
    const d25=Math.abs(actual9-seoulRule.internal),d40=Math.abs(actual9-regionalRule.internal);
    if(d25<=seoulRule.boundary){score+=120-Math.round(d25*100);reasons.push(`9등급 평균 ${actual9.toFixed(2)} · ${seoulRule.internal.toFixed(1)} 경계`);tags.push('2.5 경계');}
    if(d40<=regionalRule.boundary){score+=105-Math.round(d40*100);reasons.push(`9등급 평균 ${actual9.toFixed(2)} · ${regionalRule.internal.toFixed(1)} 경계`);tags.push('4.0 경계');}
  }
  const managementGroup=actual9!=null?uepBaseManagementGroup(actual9,base.avg5):'general';
  const inseoulInternal=managementGroup==='inseoul';
  const inseoulMock=uepMockCoreFor('inseoul',mock);
  const regionalInternal=managementGroup==='inseoul'||managementGroup==='regional';
  const regionalMock=uepMockCoreFor('regional',mock);
  const imbalance=[];
  if(inseoulInternal&&!inseoulMock){score+=90;imbalance.push('인서울 내신 우세·모의 보완');}
  else if(!inseoulInternal&&inseoulMock){score+=92;imbalance.push('인서울 모의 우세·내신 보완');}
  if(regionalInternal&&!regionalMock){score+=72;imbalance.push('지거국 내신 우세·모의 보완');}
  else if(!regionalInternal&&regionalMock){score+=74;imbalance.push('지거국 모의 우세·내신 보완');}
  if(imbalance.length){reasons.push(...imbalance);tags.push('내신↔모의 불균형');}
  const participation=Number(prep.selected||0)+Number(prep.after||0)+Number(prep.night||0)+Number(prep.reports||0);
  if(participation===0){score+=58;reasons.push('프로그램·심화학습 참여 이력 부족');tags.push('참여 부족');}
  else if(Number(prep.selected||0)===0){score+=38;reasons.push('계열 연계 선택활동 참여 부족');tags.push('선택활동 부족');}
  if(!reasons.length)return null;
  let group='growth',stage='interest';
  if(imbalance.some(x=>x.startsWith('인서울'))||tags.includes('2.5 경계')){group='inseoul';stage='growth';}
  else if(imbalance.some(x=>x.startsWith('지거국'))||
